FILE: ./device_cloaking/verify_stealth_state.py
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""verify_stealth_state.py: Host-Side Pre-Flight Stealth State Audit Suite (Gate G1).
     3	
     4	Audits system properties, SettingsProvider developer flags, network interfaces,
     5	and open ports on the physical Android test device before malware launch.
     6	Ensures zero analysis artifacts leak into the target process space.
     7	"""
     8	
     9	import argparse
    10	import os
    11	import re
    12	import shlex
    13	import subprocess
    14	import sys
    15	
    16	# Optional PyYAML support with regex fallback
    17	try:
    18	    import yaml
    19	    HAS_YAML = True
    20	except ImportError:
    21	    HAS_YAML = False
    22	
    23	
    24	def parse_lab_config(config_path):
    25	    """Parses lab_config.yaml using PyYAML or regex fallback."""
    26	    if not os.path.exists(config_path):
    27	        return {}
    28	
    29	    if HAS_YAML:
    30	        try:
    31	            with open(config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    32	                return yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
    33	        except Exception as e:
    34	            print(f"[-] Warning: Failed to parse YAML via PyYAML: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
    35	
    36	    config = {"lab_environment": {"device": {}, "telemetry": {}}}
    37	    try:
    38	        with open(config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    39	            content = f.read()
    40	        serial_m = re.search(r'serial:\s*["\']?([^"\'\s#]*)', content)
    41	        if serial_m and serial_m.group(1):
    42	            config["lab_environment"]["device"]["serial"] = serial_m.group(1)
    43	        tmp_m = re.search(r'remote_tmp_dir:\s*["\']?([^"\'\s#]*)', content)
    44	        if tmp_m and tmp_m.group(1):
    45	            config["lab_environment"]["device"]["remote_tmp_dir"] = tmp_m.group(1)
    46	    except Exception as e:
    47	        print(f"[-] Warning: Fallback config parser error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
    48	
    49	    return config
    50	
    51	
    52	class StealthAuditor:
    53	    def __init__(self, serial=None, dry_run=False, use_root=True):
    54	        self.serial = serial
    55	        self.dry_run = dry_run
    56	        self.use_root = use_root
    57	
    58	    def run_adb(self, cmd_args, check=False, as_root=False):
    59	        """Executes an ADB command with optional serial routing and root elevation."""
    60	        base = ["adb"]
    61	        if self.serial:
    62	            base.extend(["-s", self.serial])
    63	
    64	        if as_root and self.use_root:
    65	            effective_args = cmd_args[1:] if cmd_args and cmd_args[0] == "shell" else cmd_args
    66	            inner_cmd = " ".join(shlex.quote(arg) for arg in effective_args)
    67	            full_cmd = base + ["shell", "su", "-c", inner_cmd]
    68	        else:
    69	            full_cmd = base + cmd_args
    70	
    71	        if self.dry_run:
    72	            cmd_str = " ".join(cmd_args)
    73	            if "getprop ro.debuggable" in cmd_str:
    74	                return "0"
    75	            if "getprop ro.secure" in cmd_str:
    76	                return "1"
    77	            if "getprop ro.build.type" in cmd_str:
    78	                return "user"
    79	            if "getprop ro.build.tags" in cmd_str:
    80	                return "release-keys"
    81	            if "getprop sys.usb.state" in cmd_str:
    82	                return "mtp"
    83	            if "getprop sys.usb.config" in cmd_str:
    84	                return "mtp"
    85	            if "getprop init.svc.adbd" in cmd_str:
    86	                return "stopped"
    87	            if "getprop http.proxyHost" in cmd_str:
    88	                return ""
    89	            if "settings get global adb_enabled" in cmd_str:
    90	                return "0"
    91	            if "settings get global development_settings_enabled" in cmd_str:
    92	                return "0"
    93	            if "settings get global http_proxy" in cmd_str:
    94	                return ""
    95	            if "settings get global global_http_proxy_host" in cmd_str:
    96	                return ""
    97	            if "ls -1 /data/misc/user/0/cacerts-added/" in cmd_str:
    98	                return ""
    99	            if "ls -1 /data/misc/keychain/cacerts-added/" in cmd_str:
   100	                return ""
   101	            if "ip -o link show" in cmd_str:
   102	                return "1: lo: <LOOPBACK> \n2: wlan0: <BROADCAST,MULTICAST,UP> "
   103	            if "/proc/net/tcp" in cmd_str:
   104	                return "  sl  local_address rem_address   st tx_queue rx_queue tr tm->when retrnsmt   uid  timeout inode"
   105	            return "SUCCESS"
   106	
   107	        try:
   108	            res = subprocess.run(
   109	                full_cmd,
   110	                capture_output=True,
   111	                text=True,
   112	                check=check
   113	            )
   114	            return res.stdout.strip()
   115	        except FileNotFoundError:
   116	            print("[!] Error: 'adb' executable not found in PATH.", file=sys.stderr)
   117	            return ""
   118	        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
   119	            return e.stdout.strip() if e.stdout else ""
   120	        except Exception as e:
   121	            print(f"[!] ADB execution exception: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
   122	            return ""
   123	
   124	    def push_and_apply_props(self, script_path="device_cloaking/apply_stealth_props.sh"):
   125	        """Pushes apply_stealth_props.sh to device and executes it via root."""
   126	        print("[*] Pushing and applying stealth cloaking properties...")
   127	        if self.dry_run:
   128	            print(f"[DRY-RUN] adb push {script_path} /data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh")
   129	            print("[DRY-RUN] adb shell su -c 'sh /data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh apply'")
   130	            print("[+] [DRY-RUN] Stealth properties applied successfully.")
   131	            return True
   132	
   133	        if not os.path.exists(script_path):
   134	            print(f"[!] Error: Script '{script_path}' not found on host.", file=sys.stderr)
   135	            return False
   136	
   137	        # Push script to device tmp
   138	        push_out = self.run_adb(["push", script_path, "/data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh"])
   139	        self.run_adb(["shell", "chmod", "755", "/data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh"])
   140	
   141	        # Execute via su -c
   142	        out = self.run_adb(["shell", "su", "-c", "sh /data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh apply"])
   143	        print(out)
   144	        return True
   145	
   146	    def restore_props(self, script_path="device_cloaking/apply_stealth_props.sh"):
   147	        """Reverts stealth properties to standard developer debugging state."""
   148	        print("[*] Restoring standard developer debug properties on device...")
   149	        if self.dry_run:
   150	            print("[DRY-RUN] adb shell su -c 'sh /data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh restore'")
   151	            print("[+] [DRY-RUN] Properties restored to developer defaults.")
   152	            return True
   153	
   154	        self.run_adb(["push", script_path, "/data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh"])
   155	        self.run_adb(["shell", "chmod", "755", "/data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh"])
   156	        out = self.run_adb(["shell", "su", "-c", "sh /data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh restore"])
   157	        print(out)
   158	        return True
   159	
   160	    def audit(self):
   161	        """Executes full multi-layer stealth state audit."""
   162	        print("=" * 65)
   163	        print("[*] STARTING PRE-FLIGHT STEALTH STATE AUDIT (Gate G1)")
   164	        print("=" * 65)
   165	        failures = []
   166	        warnings = []
   167	
   168	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   169	        # Layer 1: System Properties via getprop
   170	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   171	        print("[*] Layer 1: Auditing System Properties (Kernel/Init Memory)...")
   172	        expected_props = {
   173	            "ro.debuggable": "0",
   174	            "ro.secure": "1",
   175	            "ro.build.type": "user",
   176	            "ro.build.tags": "release-keys",
   177	            "sys.usb.state": "mtp",
   178	            "sys.usb.config": "mtp",
   179	            "init.svc.adbd": "stopped"
   180	        }
   181	
   182	        for prop, expected in expected_props.items():
   183	            actual = self.run_adb(["shell", "getprop", prop])
   184	            if actual != expected:
   185	                failures.append(f"System Property '{prop}' is '{actual}' (expected '{expected}')")
   186	                print(f"  [-] {prop} = '{actual}' [FAIL: expected '{expected}']")
   187	            else:
   188	                print(f"  [+] {prop} = '{actual}' (PASS)")
   189	
   190	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   191	        # Layer 2: Settings.Global Developer Flags
   192	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   193	        print("[*] Layer 2: Auditing Settings.Global Developer Flags...")
   194	        adb_setting = self.run_adb(["shell", "settings", "get", "global", "adb_enabled"])
   195	        dev_setting = self.run_adb(["shell", "settings", "get", "global", "development_settings_enabled"])
   196	
   197	        if adb_setting not in ("0", "null", ""):
   198	            failures.append(f"Settings.Global.adb_enabled reports '{adb_setting}' (expected '0' or unexposed)")
   199	            print(f"  [-] Settings.Global.adb_enabled = '{adb_setting}' [FAIL]")
   200	        else:
   201	            print(f"  [+] Settings.Global.adb_enabled = '{adb_setting}' (PASS)")
   202	
   203	        if dev_setting not in ("0", "null", ""):
   204	            warnings.append(f"Settings.Global.development_settings_enabled is '{dev_setting}' (recommend '0')")
   205	            print(f"  [*] Settings.Global.development_settings_enabled = '{dev_setting}' (WARN)")
   206	        else:
   207	            print(f"  [+] Settings.Global.development_settings_enabled = '{dev_setting}' (PASS)")
   208	
   209	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   210	        # Layer 3: Network Interfaces & Proxy
   211	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   212	        print("[*] Layer 3: Auditing Network Hygiene (VPN & Proxy Interfaces)...")
   213	        link_output = self.run_adb(["shell", "ip -o link show 2>/dev/null | awk -F': ' '{print $2}'"])
   214	        links = link_output.split() if link_output else []
   215	
   216	        vpn_ifaces = [iface for iface in links if iface.startswith(("tun", "ppp", "p2p"))]
   217	        if vpn_ifaces:
   218	            for vpn in vpn_ifaces:
   219	                failures.append(f"Forbidden VPN/P2P network interface detected: '{vpn}'")
   220	                print(f"  [-] VPN Interface detected: '{vpn}' [FAIL]")
   221	        else:
   222	            clean_links = ", ".join(links) if links else "lo, wlan0"
   223	            print(f"  [+] No VPN/tun interfaces active. Active: {clean_links} (PASS)")
   224	
   225	        proxy = self.run_adb(["shell", "getprop", "http.proxyHost"])
   226	        if proxy:
   227	            failures.append(f"Android system proxy property detected: http.proxyHost='{proxy}'")
   228	            print(f"  [-] http.proxyHost = '{proxy}' [FAIL]")
   229	        else:
   230	            print("  [+] No system HTTP proxy configured (PASS)")
   231	
   232	        # --- Settings.Global http_proxy / global_http_proxy_host ---
   233	        for setting in ["http_proxy", "global_http_proxy_host"]:
   234	            proxy_setting = self.run_adb(["shell", "settings", "get", "global", setting])
   235	            if proxy_setting and proxy_setting.strip():
   236	                failures.append(f"Settings.Global.{setting} is set to '{proxy_setting.strip()}'")
   237	                print(f"  [-] Settings.Global.{setting} = '{proxy_setting.strip()}' [FAIL]")
   238	            else:
   239	                print(f"  [+] Settings.Global.{setting} is unset (PASS)")
   240	
   241	        # --- Rogue user-installed CA certificates ---
   242	        ca_dirs = ["/data/misc/user/0/cacerts-added/", "/data/misc/keychain/cacerts-added/"]
   243	        rogue_cas = []
   244	        for ca_dir in ca_dirs:
   245	            listing = self.run_adb(["shell", "ls", "-1", ca_dir], as_root=True)
   246	            if listing and "No such file" not in listing and "No such file or directory" not in listing:
   247	                certs = [c for c in listing.split() if c.strip()]
   248	                if certs:
   249	                    rogue_cas.extend(certs)
   250	                    failures.append(f"Rogue user-installed CA certificates found in {ca_dir}: {', '.join(certs)}")
   251	                    print(f"  [-] Rogue user CA certs in {ca_dir}: {len(certs)} certificate(s) [FAIL]")
   252	                else:
   253	                    print(f"  [+] No user CA certificates in {ca_dir} (PASS)")
   254	            else:
   255	                print(f"  [+] No user CA certificate directory at {ca_dir} (PASS)")
   256	        if rogue_cas:
   257	            print("    [!] Rogue user CA certificates will cause TLS pinning failure and RASP/malware detection.")
   258	
   259	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   260	        # Layer 4: Port Hygiene (Frida & Remote ADB)
   261	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   262	        print("[*] Layer 4: Auditing Port Exposure (Frida & ADB Ports)...")
   263	        tcp_dump = self.run_adb(["shell", "cat /proc/net/tcp /proc/net/tcp6 2>/dev/null"])
   264	
   265	        # 27042 in hex is 69A2
   266	        if ":69A2" in tcp_dump or "27042" in tcp_dump:
   267	            failures.append("Default Frida port 27042 (:69A2) is OPEN on device! Use stealth port 38921.")
   268	            print("  [-] Default Frida port 27042 is OPEN [FAIL]")
   269	        else:
   270	            print("  [+] Default Frida port 27042 is CLOSED (PASS)")
   271	
   272	        # 5555 in hex is 15B3 (remote TCP ADB)
   273	        if ":15B3" in tcp_dump:
   274	            warnings.append("ADB TCP port 5555 (:15B3) is open on device. Ensure isolated subnet routing.")
   275	            print("  [*] ADB TCP port 5555 is listening (WARN)")
   276	        else:
   277	            print("  [+] ADB TCP port 5555 is not exposed (PASS)")
   278	
   279	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   280	        # Layer 5: ADB Automation Viability
   281	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   282	        print("[*] Layer 5: Verifying ADB Transport Viability...")
   283	        test_uid = self.run_adb(["shell", "id -u"])
   284	        if test_uid:
   285	            print(f"  [+] ADB transport functional (caller UID={test_uid}) (PASS)")
   286	        else:
   287	            failures.append("ADB transport unresponsive after property masking.")
   288	            print("  [-] ADB transport unresponsive [FAIL]")
   289	
   290	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   291	        # Audit Summary & Gate Determination
   292	        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   293	        print("-" * 65)
   294	        if warnings:
   295	            print("[!] Operational Warnings:")
   296	            for w in warnings:
   297	                print(f"  - {w}")
   298	
   299	        if failures:
   300	            print("[!] PRE-FLIGHT STEALTH AUDIT FAILED (Gate G1 Blocked):")
   301	            for f in failures:
   302	                print(f"  [X] {f}")
   303	            print("=" * 65)
   304	            print("Action Required: Run 'python3 device_cloaking/verify_stealth_state.py --push-and-apply'")
   305	            print("or check LSPosed HideDevMode configuration before launching malware.")
   306	            return False
   307	        else:
   308	            print("[+] ALL PRE-FLIGHT STEALTH CHECKS PASSED (Gate G1 Clear).")
   309	            print("    Device is clean, masked, and authorized for malware detonation.")
   310	            print("=" * 65)
   311	            return True
   312	
   313	
   314	def main():
   315	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
   316	        description="dbug Host-Side Pre-Flight Stealth State Audit Suite (Gate G1)"
   317	    )
   318	    parser.add_argument("--config", default="config/lab_config.yaml", help="Path to lab_config.yaml")
   319	    parser.add_argument("--serial", default=None, help="ADB device serial number")
   320	    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Simulate audit checks without physical hardware")
   321	    parser.add_argument("--push-and-apply", action="store_true", help="Push and apply stealth properties via root before auditing")
   322	    parser.add_argument("--restore", action="store_true", help="Revert device properties to developer defaults")
   323	    args = parser.parse_args()
   324	
   325	    # Load configuration
   326	    cfg = parse_lab_config(args.config)
   327	    dev_cfg = cfg.get("lab_environment", {}).get("device", {})
   328	    serial = args.serial or dev_cfg.get("serial") or None
   329	    use_root = dev_cfg.get("use_root", True)
   330	
   331	    auditor = StealthAuditor(serial=serial, dry_run=args.dry_run, use_root=use_root)
   332	
   333	    if args.restore:
   334	        ok = auditor.restore_props()
   335	        sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)
   336	
   337	    if args.push_and_apply:
   338	        auditor.push_and_apply_props()
   339	
   340	    passed = auditor.audit()
   341	    sys.exit(0 if passed else 1)
   342	
   343	
   344	if __name__ == "__main__":
   345	    main()

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: ./docs/SOP.md
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1	# dbug: Standard Operating Procedures (SOP) & Analysis Runbook
     2	
     3	This document defines the official Standard Operating Procedures (SOP) for conducting dynamic analysis, threat intelligence extraction, regulatory privacy auditing, and application security penetration testing using the `dbug` framework.
     4	
     5	---
     6	
     7	## SOP-01: Laboratory Setup & Isolation
     8	
     9	### 1. Objective
    10	Establish a physically isolated, zero-trace testing environment consisting of an external Linux Wi-Fi Access Point (`ap0`) and a dedicated rooted Android test device.
    11	
    12	### 2. Pre-Requisites
    13	- Host machine running Linux (Debian/Ubuntu) or macOS connected to a Linux gateway host.
    14	- Physical Android test device (Android 12-14, ARM64) rooted via Magisk, KernelSU, or APatch, with LSPosed installed.
    15	- USB connection to test device for ADB automation.
    16	- Dedicated Wi-Fi adapter on the Linux host configured for `hostapd`.
    17	
    18	### 3. Procedure
    19	1. Initialize the isolated Wi-Fi gateway on the Linux host:
    20	   ```bash
    21	   sudo bash network_capture/gateway_setup.sh start --config config/lab_config.yaml
    22	   ```
    23	2. Verify that interface `ap0` is active and `ip_forward` is enabled:
    24	   ```bash
    25	   ip addr show ap0
    26	   cat /proc/sys/net/ipv4/ip_forward  # Must return 1
    27	   ```
    28	3. Connect the Android test device exclusively to the SSID broadcast by `ap0`. Verify the device receives an IP in the `192.168.44.0/24` subnet.
    29	4. Apply system property cloaking on the Android device:
    30	   ```bash
    31	   adb shell su -c "sh /data/local/tmp/apply_stealth_props.sh"
    32	   ```
    33	5. Run the pre-flight verification script on the host workstation:
    34	   ```bash
    35	   python3 device_cloaking/verify_stealth_state.py --config config/lab_config.yaml
    36	   ```
    37	   **STOP CONDITION:** If any pre-flight check returns `FAIL`, do not proceed with dynamic execution until remediated.
    38	
    39	---
    40	
    41	## SOP-02: Malware Threat Intelligence & C2 Extraction (`profile: malware`)
    42	
    43	### 1. Objective
    44	Analyze captured Android infostealers, banking trojans, and spyware to extract C2 infrastructure, Telegram bot tokens, exfiltrated mock data proofs, STIX 2.1 threat bundles, and defensive YARA rules.
    45	
    46	### 2. Procedure
    47	1. Seed high-entropy synthetic canary credentials on the device:
    48	   ```bash
    49	   python3 config/seed_canaries.py --seed --config config/lab_config.yaml
    50	   ```
    51	2. Verify that `sinkhole_mode` is configured appropriately in `config/lab_config.yaml`:
    52	   - Set `sinkhole_mode: true` for initial unknown samples.
    53	   - Set `sinkhole_mode: false` for active C2 infrastructure tracking.
    54	3. Launch the automated dynamic analysis session:
    55	   ```bash
    56	   python3 orchestrator.py \
    57	       --apk samples/malware_sample.apk \
    58	       --profile malware \
    59	       --duration 120
    60	   ```
    61	4. Review generated threat intelligence artifacts in `targets/<sample_sha256>/`:
    62	   - `REPORT.md`: Executive threat intelligence summary.
    63	   - `ANALYSIS.md`: Runtime behavioral findings and evidence tags.
    64	   - `IOCS.json`: Structured list of C2 domains, IPs, and bot tokens.
    65	   - `threat_report_stix21.json`: Standards-compliant STIX 2.1 threat bundle.
    66	   - `C2_PROTOCOL.md`: Reconstructed wire transport framing.
    67	   - `FAMILY_ATTRIBUTION.json`: Heuristic malware family classification.
    68	   - `Android_Infostealer_*.yar`: Defensive YARA signature.
    69	
    70	---
    71	
    72	## SOP-03: Commercial App Privacy & Ad-Tracker Audit (`profile: privacy`)
    73	
    74	### 1. Objective
    75	Audit commercial applications (e-commerce, gaming, social media) for undisclosed PII harvesting, tracking SDK bundling, and compliance with GDPR Article 5/6, CCPA, and App Store Privacy Manifest declarations.
    76	
    77	### 2. Procedure
    78	1. Plant synthetic canary PII (contacts, SMS OTPs, clipboard data) on the device:
    79	   ```bash
    80	   python3 config/seed_canaries.py --seed --config config/lab_config.yaml
    81	   ```
    82	2. Launch the privacy auditing session:
    83	   ```bash
    84	   python3 orchestrator.py \
    85	       --package com.example.commercial \
    86	       --profile privacy \
    87	       --duration 120
    88	   ```
    89	3. Inspect privacy audit deliverables in `targets/<target_id>/`:
    90	   - `PRIVACY_AUDIT.md`: Compliance report evaluating data minimization and disclosure.
    91	   - `privacy_findings.json`: Categorized tracker domains and severity levels.
    92	   - `CANARY_EXFIL_HITS.json`: Evidence of canary data sent to third-party ad networks.
    93	
    94	---
    95	
    96	## SOP-04: Commercial AppSec & Interactive Pen-Testing (`profile: appsec`)
    97	
    98	### 1. Objective
    99	Conduct penetration testing and API reverse-engineering against commercial applications with heavy RASP defenses (fintech, banking, healthcare) without triggering anti-tamper mechanisms.
   100	
   101	### 2. Procedure
   102	1. Verify device cloaking (`verify_stealth_state.py PASS`).
   103	2. Launch the session in interactive mode:
   104	   ```bash
   105	   python3 orchestrator.py \
   106	       --package com.example.fintech \
   107	       --profile appsec \
   108	       --interactive
   109	   ```
   110	3. On the physical Android test device:
   111	   - Launch the application manually if not auto-spawned.
   112	   - Complete authentication (log in with test credentials).
   113	   - Complete Multi-Factor Authentication (SMS/TOTP) if prompted.
   114	   - Exercise application views: profile, balance check, transaction history, funds transfer, settings.
   115	4. In the host workstation terminal, press `Enter` to conclude the observation window.
   116	5. The orchestrator will automatically:
   117	   - Terminate network capture and flush eBPF TLS buffers.
   118	   - Pull master secrets and decrypt all HTTPS streams.
   119	   - Audit cryptographic operations against OWASP MASVS-CRYPTO.
   120	   - Synthesize a valid OpenAPI 3.0 specification (`openapi_spec.json`).
   121	   - Generate `API_SECURITY_REPORT.md`.
   122	
   123	---
   124	
   125	## SOP-05: Burp Suite & Postman Integration Workflow
   126	
   127	### 1. Objective
   128	Import the synthesized OpenAPI 3.0 specification into vulnerability scanners and API testing suites for automated fuzzing.
   129	
   130	### 2. Burp Suite Professional Workflow:
   131	1. Locate the synthesized specification in `targets/<target_id>/openapi_spec.json`.
   132	2. In Burp Suite, open the Extender tab and install **OpenAPI Parser** (from the BApp Store).
   133	3. Under the Extensions tab, select OpenAPI Parser and click **Browse** to load `openapi_spec.json`.
   134	4. Burp Suite will parse all routes, parameters, and headers directly into the Target Site Map.
   135	5. Send target endpoints to **Burp Scanner** for active audit or **Burp Intruder** for authorization fuzzing.
   136	
   137	### 3. Postman Workflow:
   138	1. Open Postman and click **Import**.
   139	2. Drag and drop `openapi_spec.json`.
   140	3. Select **Import as Collection with API**.
   141	4. Configure collection-level environment variables (e.g., `{{baseUrl}}`, `{{token}}`).
   142	5. Execute automated test runs using Postman Runner / Newman.
   143	
   144	---
   145	
   146	## SOP-06: Post-Analysis Teardown & Canary Hygiene
   147	
   148	### 1. Objective
   149	Ensure zero residual state, clean up synthetic credentials, and prevent artifact cross-contamination between test sessions.
   150	
   151	### 2. Procedure
   152	1. Force-stop and uninstall the target application:
   153	   ```bash
   154	   adb uninstall <package_name>
   155	   ```
   156	2. Purge all synthetic canary contacts, SMS messages, and clipboard text:
   157	   ```bash
   158	   python3 config/seed_canaries.py --clean --config config/lab_config.yaml
   159	   ```
   160	3. Remove temporary staging binaries from the test device:
   161	   ```bash
   162	   adb shell "rm -f /data/local/tmp/sample.apk /data/local/tmp/sslkey.log /data/local/tmp/tls_decrypted.pcapng"
   163	   ```
   164	4. Verify all output artifacts are safely archived in `sessions/<session_id>/` and `targets/<target_id>/`.

FILE: ./docs/steps.md

     1	# Steps Log: dbug Engineering & Analysis Ledger
     2	
     3	All engineering choices, tool selections, and analysis decisions are logged chronologically.
     4	Format conforms to `dbug-step-rationale`. Timestamps are in Asia/Saigon (+07:00).
     5	
     6	---
     7	
     8	## 2026-10-16
     9	
    10	- **Step:** Initialized framework documentation, roadmap structure, and task initialization plan.
    11	  **Decision:** Standardized on `task-init.md`, `REPO_ROADMAP.md`, `malware_debugger.md`, and `.claude/skills/`; rejected: unstructured ad-hoc implementation without gate tracking.
    12	  **Rationale:** Analysis of evasive Android malware requires strict operational discipline, pre-flight gate enforcement, and zero analysis leakage.
    13	  **Evidence:** [C] Repository mandate in `AGENTS.md`.
    14	  **Revisit if:** Repository structure or gate definitions are updated.
    15	
    16	- **Step:** Phase 1 implementation kickoff - Configuration & Canary Data Seeding (`config/`).
    17	  **Decision:** Created declarative `config/lab_config.yaml`, high-entropy `config/mock_canaries.json`, and automated `config/seed_canaries.py` with multi-mechanism fallback (root `su -c` and unprivileged shell, multi-provider clipboard injection, and `--dry-run` simulation mode).
    18	  **Decision:** Adopted strict `CANARY_` prefixed tokens with high-entropy identifiers (e.g., `CANARY_SMS_OTP_9921`, `0xCANARY...`); rejected: generic or realistic-only strings lacking unique canary prefixes.
    19	  **Rationale:** Generic fake strings (e.g., standard phone numbers or random words) cause false-positive matches when scanning network dumps or decompiled trees. High-entropy prefixed strings guarantee 100% precision during correlation.
    20	  **Evidence:** [C] Schema and correlation engine requirements in `dbug-canary-seeding` and `dbug-canary-correlation`.
    21	  **Revisit if:** Target sample specifically implements heuristic string checks filtering out strings containing "CANARY" (if detected, rotate prefix to randomized high-entropy hex markers).
    22	
    23	- **Step:** Phase 2 implementation - On-Device Stealth Cloaking (`device_cloaking/`).
    24	  **Decision:** Implemented `device_cloaking/apply_stealth_props.sh` using root `resetprop` in memory (`ro.debuggable=0`, `ro.secure=1`, `sys.usb.state=mtp`, `sys.usb.config=mtp`, `init.svc.adbd=stopped`) with `apply`, `restore`, `status`, and `verify` modes.
    25	  **Decision:** Kept `adbd` transport alive via USB/TCP while masking user-space properties via `resetprop init.svc.adbd stopped`; rejected: shutting down `adbd` or using standard Developer Options unmasked.
    26	  **Rationale:** Infostealers query `ro.debuggable`, `sys.usb.state`, and `init.svc.adbd` to determine if ADB is attached. Masking properties in memory allows continuous host automation while presenting stock production state to the target process.
    27	  **Evidence:** [C] Threat model in `malware_debugger.md` line 28; [C] anti-analysis cues in `dbug-device-cloaking`.
    28	  **Revisit if:** Target sample executes native code inspecting physical hardware descriptors or USB bus descriptors directly via sysfs.
    29	
    30	- **Step:** Implemented `device_cloaking/check_lsposed_devmode.sh` for system_server SettingsProvider audit.
    31	  **Decision:** Verified `Settings.Global.adb_enabled` and `development_settings_enabled` at the system_server IPC layer (LSPosed `HideDevMode`); rejected: injecting in-process Frida hooks into malware process to fake settings.
    32	  **Rationale:** In-process hooks in the malware address space alter memory layouts and can be fingerprinted by anti-hooking heuristics or watchdog threads. Spoofing at the `system_server` Binder provider level leaves zero process artifacts in the malware.
    33	  **Evidence:** [C] `dbug-device-cloaking` Section 2; [C] `AGENTS.md` stealth hierarchy rule 3.
    34	  **Revisit if:** Device runs KernelSU/APatch without Zygisk/LSPosed installed (fallback to in-process hook `bypass_antidebug.js`).
    35	
    36	- **Step:** Implemented 5-layer pre-flight audit suite in `device_cloaking/verify_stealth_state.py`.
    37	  **Decision:** Automated checks across system properties, SettingsProvider flags, VPN/tun network interfaces, system proxy properties, and default Frida TCP port 27042 (`:69A2`), with automated `--push-and-apply`, `--restore`, and `--dry-run` modes.
    38	  **Rationale:** Pre-flight gate G1 enforcement prevents accidental malware detonation in an unmasked environment, eliminating wasted analysis runs and adversary evasion triggers.
    39	  **Evidence:** [C] Gate G1 requirements in `AGENTS.md` and `REPO_ROADMAP.md`.
    40	  **Revisit if:** Android 15+ deprecates or refactors getprop interfaces or introduces new framework anti-debug APIs.
    41	
    42	- **Step:** Implemented `network_capture/gateway_setup.sh` for zero-footprint network capture.
    43	  **Decision:** Configured transparent external Linux Wi-Fi AP (`ap0`) with iptables NAT masquerade and raw `tcpdump` capture (`-s 0 -w raw_traffic.pcap`); rejected: on-device VPN capture apps (PCAPdroid, Charles) or setting Wi-Fi HTTP proxies.
    44	  **Rationale:** Infostealers query `http.proxyHost` and inspect network interfaces for `tun0` or `ppp0`. External routing at the gateway host leaves zero on-device artifacts or transport flags.
    45	  **Evidence:** [C] `malware_debugger.md` lines 16-17; [C] `dbug-network-gateway` section 1.
    46	  **Revisit if:** Analyst host workstation lacks secondary 802.11 nl80211 AP adapter (use dedicated Linux AP router or routed bridge).
    47	
    48	- **Step:** Implemented `network_capture/ecapture_runner.py` for out-of-band TLS plaintext capture.
    49	  **Decision:** Kernel eBPF uprobes on `/apex/com.android.conscrypt/lib64/libssl.so` via static ARM64 `ecapture` on `wlan0`, generating `sslkey.log` and `tls_decrypted.pcapng`; rejected: installing user CA certificates into Android trust store.
    50	  **Rationale:** Infostealers enforce TLS certificate pinning (OkHttp CertificatePinner) and terminate execution if an unrecognized CA is detected. eBPF uprobes extract plaintext and master secrets directly from kernel memory without touching the malware address space.
    51	  **Evidence:** [C] `dbug-ebpf-capture` section 1; [E] eBPF uprobes on `SSL_read`/`SSL_write`.
    52	  **Revisit if:** Target sample statically links custom BoringSSL/OpenSSL into its own native ELF library instead of using Conscrypt (fallback to in-process Frida hooks).
    53	
    54	- **Step:** Implemented in-process fallback stealth cloak in `instrumentation/agent/bypass_antidebug.js`.
    55	  **Decision:** Surgical hooks on `Settings.Global.getInt`, `Debug.isDebuggerConnected()`, and `__system_property_get`; rejected: blanket hooking of all Java reflection or classloaders.
    56	  **Rationale:** Heavy-handed hooks introduce timing anomalies that trip anti-analysis watchdogs. Surgical interception addresses specific evasion checks without bloating call stacks.
    57	  **Evidence:** [C] Threat model in `dbug-anti-analysis` section 1; [C] `instrumentation/agent/bypass_antidebug.js`.
    58	  **Revisit if:** Target sample performs anti-hooking scans against memory maps (`/proc/self/maps` for `frida-agent.so`).
    59	
    60	- **Step:** Implemented pre-network crypto extraction in `instrumentation/agent/crypto_dumper.js`.
    61	  **Decision:** Intercepted `javax.crypto.Cipher.init()` and `Cipher.doFinal()`, extracting algorithm, secret key bytes, IV bytes, plaintext input, and ciphertext output; rejected: reverse engineering custom obfuscated cipher algorithms statically.
    62	  **Rationale:** Dynamic extraction at the `doFinal()` bottleneck captures the payload in cleartext regardless of whether the cipher was AES, DES, RC4, or multi-round XOR.
    63	  **Evidence:** [M] Memory hook output in `instrumentation_events.jsonl`; [C] `dbug-frida-instrumentation`.
    64	  **Revisit if:** Malware executes proprietary native encryption in C/C++ without invoking standard Java `Cipher` APIs (fallback to native `send()` or Unicorn emulation).
    65	
    66	- **Step:** Implemented low-level socket tracer in `instrumentation/agent/socket_tracer.js` and loader in `instrumentation/frida_loader.py`.
    67	  **Decision:** Hooked native `libc.so` `connect()`, `send()`, and `sendto()`, streaming telemetry over non-standard Frida port 38921; rejected: default port 27042.
    68	  **Rationale:** Catches raw non-HTTP socket C2 beacons while masking the instrumentation port from local port enumerations.
    69	  **Evidence:** [C] `malware_debugger.md` lines 28-29; [M] `instrumentation_events.jsonl`.
    70	  **Revisit if:** Malware detects Frida through thread name checks or anonymous executable page scanning.
    71	
    72	- **Step:** Implemented TLS packet decryptor in `parser_correlator/pcap_decryptor.py`.
    73	  **Decision:** Integrated `tshark` TLS keylog decryption (`-o tls.keylog_file:<keylog>`) with fallback simulated flows; rejected: manual packet reassembly or proxy logs.
    74	  **Rationale:** Out-of-band TLS master secrets (`sslkey.log`) captured via eBPF enable 100% deterministic decryption of raw Wi-Fi gateway frames without device certificates.
    75	  **Evidence:** [E] eBPF NSS master secret; [N] raw L3/L4 gateway PCAP frames.
    76	  **Revisit if:** Target malware utilizes non-TLS proprietary protocol over custom TCP port.
    77	
    78	- **Step:** Implemented multi-encoding canary correlation engine in `parser_correlator/canary_hunter.py`.
    79	  **Decision:** Automated scanning across raw, Base64, Hexadecimal, and URL-encoded variants against both memory dumps and decrypted network flows; rejected: single-encoding substring searches.
    80	  **Rationale:** Modern infostealers transform harvested credentials (Base64 encoding, hex packing) before dispatching them over HTTP POST or Telegram bot APIs. Multi-encoding permutations eliminate false negatives.
    81	  **Evidence:** [M] Pre-encryption buffer matches; [N] decrypted network payload matches in `CANARY_EXFIL_HITS.json`.
    82	  **Revisit if:** Malware compresses data (gzip, zlib, brotli) prior to custom encryption (add decompression pre-processor).
    83	
    84	- **Step:** Implemented automated threat intelligence extractor in `parser_correlator/ioc_extractor.py` and master workflow in `orchestrator.py`.
    85	  **Decision:** Normalized indicators into machine-readable `iocs.json`, STIX 2.1 JSON bundle (`threat_report_stix21.json`), and evidence-tagged executive Markdown report (`analysis_report.md`).
    86	  **Rationale:** Converts raw sandbox telemetry into standardized, publication-grade threat intelligence deliverables for defensive analysts and automated SIEM ingestion.
    87	  **Evidence:** [C] `dbug-threat-intel` specification; [C] STIX 2.1 schema compliance.
    88	  **Revisit if:** STIX 2.1 schema is superseded or custom TAXII feed format is required.
    89	
    90	- **Step:** Implemented Gate G0 static triage engine in `sample_triage/triage_apk.py`.
    91	  **Decision:** Automated safe pre-detonation static assessment parsing APK hashes, permissions, accessibility services, packers, anti-analysis strings, and hardcoded C2s without executing untrusted code on the host; rejected: launching dynamic analysis blindly without static profiling.
    92	  **Rationale:** Static triage identifies evasive heuristics, packers, and architecture before physical device spawn, ensuring cloaking countermeasures are armed appropriately.
    93	  **Evidence:** [C] `dbug-sample-triage` specification; [C] `sample_triage/triage_apk.py`.
    94	  **Revisit if:** Target sample is heavily protected by commercial VMP (virtual machine protector) requiring dynamic Dalvik bytecode dumping.
    95	
    96	- **Step:** Implemented defensive YARA signature generator in `parser_correlator/yara_generator.py`.
    97	  **Decision:** Automatically synthesized YARA detection rules from observed C2 domains, Telegram tokens, and package identifiers; rejected: manual manual YARA authoring.
    98	  **Rationale:** Enables immediate defensive perimeter deployment and retro-hunting across organizational telemetry.
    99	  **Evidence:** [C] AGENTS.md ground rule 4; [C] `parser_correlator/yara_generator.py`.
   100	  **Revisit if:** Generated YARA rule produces false positives on standard library dependencies (adjust condition to require 2+ unique C2 strings).
   101	
   102	- **Step:** Enhanced in-process cloaking in `instrumentation/agent/bypass_antidebug.js` with ptrace and anti-root file access interceptors.
   103	  **Decision:** Hooked native `ptrace(PTRACE_TRACEME)` returning 0, hooked `access()` spoofing -1 for `/su` checks, and hooked `kill()` to trace self-termination attempts.
   104	  **Rationale:** Evasive infostealers attempt self-ptrace to detect attached debuggers and self-terminate upon finding `/system/bin/su`.
   105	  **Evidence:** [C] `dbug-anti-analysis` section 1; [M] in-process libc interceptors.
   106	  **Revisit if:** Malware invokes direct raw syscalls (`svc 0`) bypassing `libc.so` stubs.
   107	
   108	- **Step:** Implemented automated C2 protocol framing & specification reconstructor in `parser_correlator/c2_protocol_decoder.py`.
   109	  **Decision:** Analyzed decrypted HTTP/TLS flows, WebSocket headers, and socket streams to reconstruct wire layout, magic bytes, length prefix, serialization format, and tasking opcodes into `C2_PROTOCOL.md`; rejected: manual packet inspection.
   110	  **Rationale:** Accelerates reverse engineering and enables reproducible C2 emulation and signature generation.
   111	  **Evidence:** [N] Network captures; [M] native socket arguments.
   112	  **Revisit if:** Target sample communicates over custom raw UDP protocol.
   113	
   114	- **Step:** Implemented behavioral malware family attribution engine in `parser_correlator/family_classifier.py`.
   115	  **Decision:** Evaluated observed permissions, anti-analysis checks, C2 transports, and crypto algorithms against the `dbug-infostealer-catalog` (Ermac, Hook, CraxsRAT, SpyNote, Godfather, Octo), outputting confidence-scored `FAMILY_ATTRIBUTION.json`.
   116	  **Rationale:** Connects low-level execution artifacts to strategic adversary profiling and MITRE ATT&CK mappings.
   117	  **Evidence:** [C] Permissions and DEX cues; [M] crypto algorithm; [N] transport signatures.
   118	  **Revisit if:** Novel or hybrid malware families emerge that do not match existing catalog profiles.
   119	
   120	- **Step:** Implemented defensive AArch64 native string decryptor and JNI emulation harness in `sample_triage/native_emulator.py`.
   121	  **Decision:** Built native emulator supporting Unicorn engine with a pure-Python fallback for headless CI and dry-run environments to decrypt obfuscated strings in `.so` files; rejected: forcing physical device execution for every native string routine.
   122	  **Rationale:** Advanced infostealers store C2 URLs and tokens inside packed native `.so` libraries that cannot be decompiled by standard Dalvik decompiler tools (`jadx`/`apktool`).
   123	  **Evidence:** [C] Native `.so` ELF segments; [C] decompiled JNI strings.
   124	  **Revisit if:** Target library depends on extensive proprietary native Android OS frameworks outside libc.
   125	
   126	- **Step:** Synchronized canonical sample workspace `targets/<sample_hash>/` in `orchestrator.py`.

   128	  **Rationale:** Strict compliance with `AGENTS.md` directory structure ensures reproducible and auditable analysis packages.
   129	  **Evidence:** [C] Workspace layout in `AGENTS.md`.
   130	  **Revisit if:** Target document schema is updated.
   131	
   132	- **Step:** Implemented comprehensive automated end-to-end test suite in `tests/test_framework_e2e.py`.
   133	  **Decision:** Implemented 12 automated unit and integration tests verifying all 6 gates (G0-G5) across seeding, cloaking, capture, instrumentation, decryption, correlation, and attribution; rejected: manual interactive testing only.
   134	  **Rationale:** Guarantees regression protection and testability across development workstations and continuous integration pipelines.
   135	  **Evidence:** [C] Test suite test runner output in `tests/test_framework_e2e.py`.
   136	  **Revisit if:** New pipeline stages or CLI options are introduced.
   137	
   138	- **Step:** Implemented curated mobile tracking and analytics taxonomy in `config/tracker_catalog.json`.
   139	  **Decision:** Standardized on signatures for top 10 advertising, attribution, and analytics networks (Meta Graph, Firebase, AppsFlyer, Adjust, Kochava, TikTok Pangle, Branch, Mixpanel, Sentry, Unity Ads); rejected: unstructured domain lists without risk ratings.
   140	  **Rationale:** Distinguishing First-Party API backends from Third-Party ad trackers enables automated compliance audits under GDPR and CCPA.
   141	  **Evidence:** [C] `config/tracker_catalog.json`.
   142	  **Revisit if:** New advertising networks or regional tracking SDKs (e.g., in Asia/EU markets) are observed.
   143	
   144	- **Step:** Implemented mobile app privacy & data leakage auditor in `parser_correlator/privacy_auditor.py`.
   145	  **Decision:** Automated correlation of decrypted flows and canary hits against the tracker taxonomy, outputting `PRIVACY_AUDIT.md` and `privacy_findings.json`; rejected: manual log inspection for privacy auditing.
   146	  **Rationale:** Enables immediate regulatory compliance reporting (GDPR Article 5 Data Minimization, CCPA Notice Requirements, and App Store Privacy Manifest audits) for commercial apps.
   147	  **Evidence:** [N] Decrypted network flows; [M] canary correlation hits.
   148	  **Revisit if:** Target application uses encrypted proprietary protobuf tracking envelopes.

   152	  **Rationale:** Directly bridges out-of-band mobile packet captures with penetration testing tools (Postman, Burp Suite, OWASP ZAP) without breaking certificate pinning.
   153	  **Evidence:** [M] `Cipher.init` parameters; [N] decrypted REST/GraphQL flows.

   158	  **Rationale:** Elevates `dbug` into a universal mobile security platform for both adversarial threat intelligence and defensive commercial application auditing.
   159	  **Evidence:** [C] `orchestrator.py` CLI interface; [C] `tests/test_framework_e2e.py` (16 tests pass).

   163	  **Decision:** Formalized a triple-track operational architecture (`malware`, `privacy`, `appsec`, `all`) that leverages the exact same zero-trace out-of-band capture and cloaking foundation to evade both malware evasion checks and commercial RASP engines; rejected: treating non-malware as a peripheral plugin.
   164	  **Rationale:** Commercial banking, fintech, and enterprise apps use the exact same anti-tamper and anti-debugging checks as infostealers; an out-of-band architecture solves interception for both domains identically.
   165	  **Evidence:** [C] `task-non-malware.md` architecture specification.

   170	  **Rationale:** Ensures any autonomous agent or human analyst operating `dbug` understands the complete operational boundaries across malware threat intelligence, privacy auditing, and interactive penetration testing.
   171	  **Evidence:** [C] `AGENTS.md`, `REPO_ROADMAP.md`, `README.md`, `docs/SOP.md`.

   176	  **Rationale:** Delivers clear, professional executive reports appropriate for regulatory auditors and AppSec stakeholders without irrelevant malware C2 terminology.
   177	  **Evidence:** [C] `orchestrator.py`; [D] verified via `--dry-run` across all profiles.

     1	/**
     2	 * bypass_antidebug.js: In-process fallback stealth cloak for dbug.
     3	 * Intercepts Java Settings.Global, Debugger checks, and libc system properties.
     4	 */
     5	
     6	Java.perform(() => {
     7	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
     8	    // 1. Spoof Settings.Global Developer & ADB Flags
     9	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10	    try {
    11	        const SettingsGlobal = Java.use("android.provider.Settings$Global");
    12	        
    13	        // Overload 1: getInt(ContentResolver cr, String name, int def)
    14	        SettingsGlobal.getInt.overload('android.content.ContentResolver', 'java.lang.String', 'int').implementation = function(cr, name, def) {
    15	            if (name === "adb_enabled" || name === "development_settings_enabled") {
    16	                return 0;
    17	            }
    18	            return this.getInt(cr, name, def);
    19	        };
    20	
    21	        // Overload 2: getInt(ContentResolver cr, String name)
    22	        SettingsGlobal.getInt.overload('android.content.ContentResolver', 'java.lang.String').implementation = function(cr, name) {
    23	            if (name === "adb_enabled" || name === "development_settings_enabled") {
    24	                return 0;
    25	            }
    26	            return this.getInt(cr, name);
    27	        };
    28	    } catch (e) {
    29	        // Class or overload may vary across custom OEM ROMs
    30	    }
    31	
    32	    try {
    33	        const SettingsSecure = Java.use("android.provider.Settings$Secure");
    34	        SettingsSecure.getInt.overload('android.content.ContentResolver', 'java.lang.String', 'int').implementation = function(cr, name, def) {
    35	            if (name === "adb_enabled" || name === "development_settings_enabled") {
    36	                return 0;
    37	            }
    38	            return this.getInt(cr, name, def);
    39	        };
    40	    } catch (e) {}
    41	
    42	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    43	    // 2. Spoof Java Framework Debugger Connection Checks
    44	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    45	    try {
    46	        const Debug = Java.use("android.os.Debug");
    47	        Debug.isDebuggerConnected.implementation = () => false;
    48	        Debug.waitingForDebugger.implementation = () => false;
    49	    } catch (e) {}
    50	});
    51	
    52	// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53	// 3. Native libc __system_property_get Interceptor
    54	// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55	(() => {
    56	    const propGet = Module.findExportByName("libc.so", "__system_property_get");
    57	    if (propGet) {
    58	        Interceptor.attach(propGet, {
    59	            onEnter(args) {
    60	                this.name = args[0].readCString();
    61	                this.valuePtr = args[1];
    62	            },
    63	            onLeave(retval) {
    64	                if (!this.name || !this.valuePtr) return;
    65	
    66	                if (this.name === "ro.debuggable") {
    67	                    this.valuePtr.writeUtf8String("0");
    68	                } else if (this.name === "ro.secure") {
    69	                    this.valuePtr.writeUtf8String("1");
    70	                } else if (this.name === "sys.usb.state" || this.name === "sys.usb.config" || this.name === "persist.sys.usb.config") {
    71	                    this.valuePtr.writeUtf8String("mtp");
    72	                } else if (this.name === "init.svc.adbd") {
    73	                    this.valuePtr.writeUtf8String("stopped");
    74	                }
    75	            }
    76	        });
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    80	    // 4. Native ptrace(PTRACE_TRACEME) Denial Interceptor
    81	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    82	    const ptracePtr = Module.findExportByName("libc.so", "ptrace");
    83	    if (ptracePtr) {
    84	        Interceptor.attach(ptracePtr, {
    85	            onEnter(args) {
    86	                this.request = args[0].toInt32();
    87	            },
    88	            onLeave(retval) {
    89	                // PTRACE_TRACEME is 0. If called, spoof return code 0 (success)
    90	                if (this.request === 0) {
    91	                    retval.replace(ptr(0));
    92	                }
    93	            }
    94	        });
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98	    // 5. Anti-Root File Existence Checks (access / faccessat)
    99	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100	    const accessPtr = Module.findExportByName("libc.so", "access");
   101	    if (accessPtr) {
   102	        Interceptor.attach(accessPtr, {
   103	            onEnter(args) {
   104	                this.path = args[0].readCString();
   105	            },
   106	            onLeave(retval) {
   107	                if (this.path && (this.path.indexOf("/su") !== -1 || this.path.indexOf("magisk") !== -1 || this.path.indexOf("busybox") !== -1)) {
   108	                    // Spoof file not found (-1)
   109	                    retval.replace(ptr(-1));
   110	                }
   111	            }
   112	        });
   113	    }
   114	
   115	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116	    // 6. Evasion Termination Watchdog (Logging self-kill attempts)
   117	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118	    const killPtr = Module.findExportByName("libc.so", "kill");
   119	    if (killPtr) {
   120	        Interceptor.attach(killPtr, {
   121	            onEnter(args) {
   122	                const pid = args[0].toInt32();
   123	                const sig = args[1].toInt32();
   124	                if (sig === 9 || sig === 15 || sig === 6) {
   125	                    send({
   126	                        event: "SELF_TERMINATION_ATTEMPT",
   127	                        target_pid: pid,
   128	                        signal: sig,
   129	                        callstack: Thread.backtrace(this.context, Backtracer.ACCURATE).map(DebugSymbol.fromAddress).join("\n")
   130	                    });
   131	                }
   132	            }
   133	        });
   134	    }
   135	})();

FILE: ./instrumentation/agent/crypto_dumper.js
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1	/**
     2	 * crypto_dumper.js: Pre-network cryptographic operation tracer for dbug.
     3	 * Intercepts javax.crypto.Cipher to dump algorithms, secret keys, IVs,
     4	 * plaintext buffers, and ciphertexts before TLS transmission.
     5	 */
     6	
     7	Java.perform(() => {
     8	    const Cipher = Java.use("javax.crypto.Cipher");
     9	    const IvParameterSpec = Java.use("javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec");
    10	
    11	    function bytesToHex(byteArray) {
    12	        if (!byteArray) return null;
    13	        let hex = [];
    14	        for (let i = 0; i < byteArray.length; i++) {
    15	            let byte = byteArray[i] & 0xFF;
    16	            hex.push((byte < 16 ? "0" : "") + byte.toString(16));
    17	        }
    18	        return hex.join("");
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    function bytesToUtf8(byteArray) {
    22	        if (!byteArray) return null;
    23	        try {
    24	            const StringCls = Java.use("java.lang.String");
    25	            return StringCls.$new(byteArray, "UTF-8").toString();
    26	        } catch (e) {
    27	            return null;
    28	        }
    29	    }
    30	
    31	    function getOpmodeName(opmode) {
    32	        switch (opmode) {
    33	            case 1: return "ENCRYPT_MODE";
    34	            case 2: return "DECRYPT_MODE";
    35	            case 3: return "WRAP_MODE";
    36	            case 4: return "UNWRAP_MODE";
    37	            default: return `OPMODE_${opmode}`;
    38	        }
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    42	    // 1. Hook Cipher.init() to capture secret keys and IVs
    43	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44	    // Overload: init(int opmode, Key key, AlgorithmParameterSpec params)
    45	    Cipher.init.overload('int', 'java.security.Key', 'java.security.spec.AlgorithmParameterSpec').implementation = function(opmode, key, params) {
    46	        let keyHex = null;
    47	        let keyAlgo = null;
    48	        let ivHex = null;
    49	
    50	        if (key) {
    51	            try {
    52	                keyAlgo = key.getAlgorithm();
    53	                keyHex = bytesToHex(key.getEncoded());
    54	            } catch (e) {}
    55	        }
    56	
    57	        if (params && params.$className === "javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec") {
    58	            try {
    59	                const ivSpec = Java.cast(params, IvParameterSpec);
    60	                ivHex = bytesToHex(ivSpec.getIV());
    61	            } catch (e) {}
    62	        }
    63	
    64	        send({
    65	            event: "CRYPTO_INIT",
    66	            algorithm: this.getAlgorithm(),
    67	            opmode: getOpmodeName(opmode),
    68	            key_algorithm: keyAlgo,
    69	            key_hex: keyHex,
    70	            iv_hex: ivHex
    71	        });
    72	
    73	        return this.init(opmode, key, params);
    74	    };
    75	
    76	    // Overload: init(int opmode, Key key)
    77	    Cipher.init.overload('int', 'java.security.Key').implementation = function(opmode, key) {
    78	        let keyHex = null;
    79	        let keyAlgo = null;
    80	
    81	        if (key) {
    82	            try {
    83	                keyAlgo = key.getAlgorithm();
    84	                keyHex = bytesToHex(key.getEncoded());
    85	            } catch (e) {}
    86	        }
    87	
    88	        send({
    89	            event: "CRYPTO_INIT",
    90	            algorithm: this.getAlgorithm(),
    91	            opmode: getOpmodeName(opmode),
    92	            key_algorithm: keyAlgo,
    93	            key_hex: keyHex,
    94	            iv_hex: null
    95	        });
    96	
    97	        return this.init(opmode, key);
    98	    };
    99	
   100	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101	    // 2. Hook Cipher.doFinal() to capture plaintext and ciphertext buffers
   102	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103	    // Overload: doFinal(byte[] input)
   104	    Cipher.doFinal.overload('[B').implementation = function(input) {
   105	        const result = this.doFinal(input);
   106	        const algo = this.getAlgorithm();
   107	
   108	        send({
   109	            event: "CRYPTO_DOFINAL",
   110	            algorithm: algo,
   111	            input_length: input ? input.length : 0,
   112	            input_hex: bytesToHex(input),
   113	            input_utf8: bytesToUtf8(input),
   114	            output_length: result ? result.length : 0,
   115	            output_hex: bytesToHex(result),
   116	            output_utf8: bytesToUtf8(result)
   117	        });
   118	
   119	        return result;
   120	    };
   121	
   122	    // Overload: doFinal(byte[] input, int inputOffset, int inputLen)
   123	    Cipher.doFinal.overload('[B', 'int', 'int').implementation = function(input, offset, len) {
   124	        const result = this.doFinal(input, offset, len);
   125	        let slicedInput = null;
   126	        if (input && len > 0) {
   127	            try {
   128	                const Arrays = Java.use("java.util.Arrays");
   129	                slicedInput = Arrays.copyOfRange(input, offset, offset + len);
   130	            } catch (e) {}
   131	        }
   132	
   133	        send({
   134	            event: "CRYPTO_DOFINAL",
   135	            algorithm: this.getAlgorithm(),
   136	            input_length: len,
   137	            input_hex: bytesToHex(slicedInput),
   138	            input_utf8: bytesToUtf8(slicedInput),
   139	            output_length: result ? result.length : 0,
   140	            output_hex: bytesToHex(result),
   141	            output_utf8: bytesToUtf8(result)
   142	        });
   143	
   144	        return result;
   145	    };
   146	});

FILE: ./instrumentation/agent/socket_tracer.js
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1	/**
     2	 * socket_tracer.js: Low-level native socket and DNS resolution tracer for dbug.
     3	 * Intercepts libc connect(), send(), sendto() and Java InetAddress/URL to catch
     4	 * direct TCP/UDP C2 beacons and DGA lookups.
     5	 */
     6	
     7	// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
     8	// 1. Native libc Socket Interceptor
     9	// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10	(() => {
    11	    function parseSockAddr(sockaddr) {
    12	        if (!sockaddr) return null;
    13	        try {
    14	            const family = sockaddr.readU16();
    15	            if (family === 2) { // AF_INET (IPv4)
    16	                const port = (sockaddr.add(2).readU8() << 8) | sockaddr.add(3).readU8();
    17	                const ip = [
    18	                    sockaddr.add(4).readU8(),
    19	                    sockaddr.add(5).readU8(),
    20	                    sockaddr.add(6).readU8(),
    21	                    sockaddr.add(7).readU8()
    22	                ].join('.');
    23	                return { family: "AF_INET", ip: ip, port: port };
    24	            } else if (family === 10) { // AF_INET6 (IPv6)
    25	                const port = (sockaddr.add(2).readU8() << 8) | sockaddr.add(3).readU8();
    26	                let parts = [];
    27	                for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 2) {
    28	                    parts.push(((sockaddr.add(8 + i).readU8() << 8) | sockaddr.add(9 + i).readU8()).toString(16));
    29	                }
    30	                return { family: "AF_INET6", ip: parts.join(':'), port: port };
    31	            }
    32	        } catch (e) {}
    33	        return null;
    34	    }
    35	
    36	    function bufferToSnippet(bufPtr, len) {
    37	        if (!bufPtr || len <= 0) return { hex: null, utf8: null };
    38	        try {
    39	            const maxLen = Math.min(len, 1024);
    40	            const bytes = bufPtr.readByteArray(maxLen);
    41	            const u8 = new Uint8Array(bytes);
    42	            let hex = [];
    43	            let str = "";
    44	            for (let i = 0; i < u8.length; i++) {
    45	                let b = u8[i];
    46	                hex.push((b < 16 ? "0" : "") + b.toString(16));
    47	                str += (b >= 32 && b <= 126) ? String.fromCharCode(b) : ".";
    48	            }
    49	            return { hex: hex.join(""), text: str, total_len: len };
    50	        } catch (e) {
    51	            return { hex: null, text: null, total_len: len };
    52	        }
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    const connectPtr = Module.findExportByName("libc.so", "connect");
    56	    if (connectPtr) {
    57	        Interceptor.attach(connectPtr, {
    58	            onEnter(args) {
    59	                const addr = parseSockAddr(args[1]);
    60	                if (addr) {
    61	                    send({
    62	                        event: "SOCKET_CONNECT",
    63	                        family: addr.family,
    64	                        destination_ip: addr.ip,
    65	                        destination_port: addr.port
    66	                    });
    67	                }
    68	            }
    69	        });
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    const sendPtr = Module.findExportByName("libc.so", "send");
    73	    if (sendPtr) {
    74	        Interceptor.attach(sendPtr, {
    75	            onEnter(args) {
    76	                const len = args[2].toInt32();
    77	                if (len > 0) {
    78	                    const snippet = bufferToSnippet(args[1], len);
    79	                    send({
    80	                        event: "SOCKET_SEND",
    81	                        length: len,
    82	                        payload_hex: snippet.hex,
    83	                        payload_ascii: snippet.text
    84	                    });
    85	                }
    86	            }
    87	        });
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    const sendtoPtr = Module.findExportByName("libc.so", "sendto");
    91	    if (sendtoPtr) {
    92	        Interceptor.attach(sendtoPtr, {
    93	            onEnter(args) {
    94	                const len = args[2].toInt32();
    95	                const addr = parseSockAddr(args[4]);
    96	                if (len > 0) {
    97	                    const snippet = bufferToSnippet(args[1], len);
    98	                    send({
    99	                        event: "SOCKET_SENDTO",
   100	                        length: len,
   101	                        destination_ip: addr ? addr.ip : null,
   102	                        destination_port: addr ? addr.port : null,
   103	                        payload_hex: snippet.hex,
   104	                        payload_ascii: snippet.text
   105	                    });
   106	                }
   107	            }
   108	        });
   109	    }
   110	})();
   111	
   112	// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   113	// 2. Java Framework DNS & URL Tracer
   114	// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115	Java.perform(() => {
   116	    try {
   117	        const InetAddress = Java.use("java.net.InetAddress");
   118	        InetAddress.getByName.overload('java.lang.String').implementation = function(host) {
   119	            send({
   120	                event: "DNS_LOOKUP",
   121	                hostname: host
   122	            });
   123	            return this.getByName(host);
   124	        };
   125	    } catch (e) {}
   126	
   127	    try {
   128	        const URL = Java.use("java.net.URL");
   129	        URL.$init.overload('java.lang.String').implementation = function(spec) {
   130	            send({
   131	                event: "HTTP_URL_INIT",
   132	                url: spec
   133	            });
   134	            return this.$init(spec);
   135	        };
   136	    } catch (e) {}
   137	});

FILE: ./instrumentation/frida_loader.py
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""frida_loader.py: Stealth Dynamic Instrumentation Loader for dbug (Gate G4).
     3	
     4	Spawns the target APK via a non-standard Frida port (38921), compiles and injects
     5	consolidated stealth hooks (bypass_antidebug, crypto_dumper, socket_tracer), and
     6	streams structured JSON telemetry to a timestamped session log.
     7	"""
     8	
     9	import argparse
    10	import json
    11	import os
    12	import re
    13	import signal
    14	import sys
    15	import time
    16	
    17	# Optional frida import
    18	try:
    19	    import frida
    20	    HAS_FRIDA = True
    21	except ImportError:
    22	    HAS_FRIDA = False
    23	
    24	# Optional PyYAML support with regex fallback
    25	try:
    26	    import yaml
    27	    HAS_YAML = True
    28	except ImportError:
    29	    HAS_YAML = False
    30	
    31	
    32	def parse_lab_config(config_path):
    33	    """Parses lab_config.yaml using PyYAML or regex fallback."""
    34	    if not os.path.exists(config_path):
    35	        return {}
    36	
    37	    if HAS_YAML:
    38	        try:
    39	            with open(config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    40	                return yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
    41	        except Exception as e:
    42	            print(f"[-] Warning: Failed to parse YAML via PyYAML: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
    43	
    44	    config = {"lab_environment": {"device": {}, "target_sample": {}, "telemetry": {"frida": {}}}}
    45	    try:
    46	        with open(config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    47	            content = f.read()
    48	        pkg_m = re.search(r'package_name:\s*["\']?([^"\'\s#]*)', content)
    49	        if pkg_m and pkg_m.group(1):
    50	            config["lab_environment"]["target_sample"]["package_name"] = pkg_m.group(1)
    51	        port_m = re.search(r'port:\s*(\d+)', content)
    52	        if port_m:
    53	            config["lab_environment"]["telemetry"]["frida"]["port"] = int(port_m.group(1))
    54	    except Exception as e:
    55	        print(f"[-] Warning: Fallback config parser error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
    56	
    57	    return config
    58	
    59	
    60	class FridaLoader:
    61	    def __init__(self, package_name, port=38921, host="127.0.0.1",
    62	                 log_path="instrumentation_events.jsonl", dry_run=False):
    63	        self.package_name = package_name
    64	        self.port = port
    65	        self.host = host
    66	        self.log_path = log_path
    67	        self.dry_run = dry_run
    68	        self.session = None
    69	        self.device = None
    70	        self.pid = None
    71	        self.log_file = None
    72	
    73	    def compile_agent_sources(self, script_paths):
    74	        """Concatenates multiple agent JavaScript files into a single bundle."""
    75	        bundle = []
    76	        for path in script_paths:
    77	            if os.path.exists(path):
    78	                with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    79	                    bundle.append(f"// --- Begin: {path} ---\n" + f.read() + f"\n// --- End: {path} ---")
    80	            else:
    81	                print(f"[-] Warning: Agent script not found: {path}", file=sys.stderr)
    82	        return "\n\n".join(bundle)
    83	
    84	    def on_message(self, message, data):
    85	        """Callback processing messages from in-process Frida agents."""
    86	        now_ms = int(time.time() * 1000)
    87	        if message.get("type") == "send":
    88	            payload = message.get("payload", {})
    89	            payload["timestamp_ms"] = now_ms
    90	
    91	            if self.log_file:
    92	                self.log_file.write(json.dumps(payload) + "\n")
    93	                self.log_file.flush()
    94	
    95	            event = payload.get("event", "EVENT")
    96	            if event == "CRYPTO_DOFINAL":
    97	                algo = payload.get("algorithm", "UNKNOWN")
    98	                in_len = payload.get("input_length", 0)
    99	                print(f"  [CRYPTO] Cipher.doFinal({algo}) | In: {in_len}B | KeyHex: {payload.get('key_hex', 'N/A')[:16]}...")
   100	            elif event == "SOCKET_CONNECT":
   101	                dst = payload.get("destination_ip", "0.0.0.0")
   102	                port = payload.get("destination_port", 0)
   103	                print(f"  [SOCKET] connect() -> {dst}:{port}")
   104	            elif event == "DNS_LOOKUP":
   105	                print(f"  [DNS] InetAddress.getByName({payload.get('hostname')})")
   106	            else:
   107	                print(f"  [{event}] {json.dumps(payload)[:100]}")
   108	
   109	        elif message.get("type") == "error":
   110	            print(f"[!] Frida In-Process Error: {message.get('description')}", file=sys.stderr)
   111	
   112	    def run_dry_run(self, duration=5):
   113	        """Simulates instrumented execution and generates mock telemetry."""
   114	        print(f"[+] [DRY-RUN] Connecting to Frida daemon at {self.host}:{self.port}...")
   115	        print(f"[+] [DRY-RUN] Spawning target package '{self.package_name}'...")
   116	        print(f"[+] [DRY-RUN] Injected stealth agents: bypass_antidebug, crypto_dumper, socket_tracer")
   117	        print(f"[*] [DRY-RUN] Simulating {duration} seconds of malware telemetry...")
   118	
   119	        # Generate sample events
   120	        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(self.log_path)), exist_ok=True)
   121	        now_ms = int(time.time() * 1000)
   122	        sample_events = [
   123	            {
   124	                "event": "DNS_LOOKUP",
   125	                "hostname": "c2-api.malicious-gate.example",
   126	                "timestamp_ms": now_ms + 100
   127	            },
   128	            {
   129	                "event": "SOCKET_CONNECT",
   130	                "family": "AF_INET",
   131	                "destination_ip": "198.51.100.24",
   132	                "destination_port": 8443,
   133	                "timestamp_ms": now_ms + 250
   134	            },
   135	            {
   136	                "event": "CRYPTO_INIT",
   137	                "algorithm": "AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding",
   138	                "opmode": "ENCRYPT_MODE",
   139	                "key_algorithm": "AES",
   140	                "key_hex": "4a8f9c01823901b2c3d4e5f6071829304a8f9c01823901b2c3d4e5f607182930",
   141	                "iv_hex": "00112233445566778899aabbccddeeff",
   142	                "timestamp_ms": now_ms + 500
   143	            },
   144	            {
   145	                "event": "CRYPTO_DOFINAL",
   146	                "algorithm": "AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding",
   147	                "input_length": 98,
   148	                "input_hex": "596f7572206f6e652d74696d6520617574686f72697a6174696f6e20636f64652069732039383432313720666f72207472616e73616374696f6e2054582d43414e4152592d3838333932312e",
   149	                "input_utf8": "Your one-time authorization code is 984217 for transaction TX-CANARY-883921.",
   150	                "output_length": 112,
   151	                "output_hex": "a1b2c3d4e5f6071829304a8f9c018239a1b2c3d4e5f6071829304a8f9c018239",
   152	                "timestamp_ms": now_ms + 750
   153	            }
   154	        ]
   155	
   156	        with open(self.log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   157	            for ev in sample_events:
   158	                f.write(json.dumps(ev) + "\n")
   159	                print(f"  [{ev['event']}] Simulated telemetry event logged.")
   160	
   161	        print(f"[+] [DRY-RUN] Telemetry logged to {self.log_path}")
   162	        return True
   163	
   164	    def run(self, script_paths, duration=120):
   165	        """Spawns target app, injects hooks, and streams telemetry."""
   166	        if self.dry_run or not HAS_FRIDA:
   167	            if not HAS_FRIDA and not self.dry_run:
   168	                print("[-] Notice: 'frida' Python module not installed. Falling back to simulation mode.", file=sys.stderr)
   169	            return self.run_dry_run(duration=min(duration, 5))
   170	
   171	        print(f"[*] Connecting to Frida daemon at {self.host}:{self.port}...")
   172	        try:
   173	            mgr = frida.get_device_manager()
   174	            self.device = mgr.add_remote_device(f"{self.host}:{self.port}")
   175	        except Exception as e:
   176	            print(f"[!] Failed to connect to Frida remote device: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
   177	            return False
   178	
   179	        print(f"[*] Spawning target package '{self.package_name}'...")
   180	        try:
   181	            self.pid = self.device.spawn([self.package_name])
   182	            self.session = self.device.attach(self.pid)
   183	        except Exception as e:
   184	            print(f"[!] Failed to spawn package '{self.package_name}': {e}", file=sys.stderr)
   185	            return False
   186	
   187	        source = self.compile_agent_sources(script_paths)
   188	        print(f"[*] Injected consolidated agent ({len(source)} bytes)...")
   189	
   190	        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(self.log_path)), exist_ok=True)
   191	        self.log_file = open(self.log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8")
   192	
   193	        script = self.session.create_script(source)
   194	        script.on("message", self.on_message)
   195	        script.load()
   196	
   197	        # Resume process execution
   198	        self.device.resume(self.pid)
   199	        print(f"[+] Target process {self.pid} resumed. Monitoring for {duration} seconds...")
   200	
   201	        try:
   202	            time.sleep(duration)
   203	        except KeyboardInterrupt:
   204	            print("\n[!] Execution interrupted by user.")
   205	        finally:
   206	            self.cleanup()
   207	
   208	        print(f"[+] Session complete. Telemetry persisted to {self.log_path}")
   209	        return True
   210	
   211	    def cleanup(self):
   212	        """Cleans up session, terminates target process, and flushes log file."""
   213	        if self.log_file and not self.log_file.closed:
   214	            self.log_file.close()
   215	
   216	        if self.session:
   217	            try:
   218	                self.session.detach()
   219	            except Exception:
   220	                pass
   221	
   222	        if self.device and self.pid:
   223	            try:
   224	                self.device.kill(self.pid)
   225	            except Exception:
   226	                pass
   227	
   228	
   229	def main():
   230	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
   231	        description="dbug Stealth Frida Runtime Instrumentation Loader (Gate G4)"
   232	    )
   233	    parser.add_argument("--apk", default=None, help="Target APK path (extracts package name)")
   234	    parser.add_argument("--package", default=None, help="Target Android package name")
   235	    parser.add_argument("--config", default="config/lab_config.yaml", help="Path to lab_config.yaml")
   236	    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=None, help="Frida remote TCP port (default: 38921)")
   237	    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=int, default=120, help="Observation window in seconds")
   238	    parser.add_argument("--output", default="./sessions/current_capture/instrumentation_events.jsonl", help="Telemetry output log")
   239	    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Simulate execution without physical hardware")
   240	    args = parser.parse_args()
   241	
   242	    cfg = parse_lab_config(args.config)
   243	    sample_cfg = cfg.get("lab_environment", {}).get("target_sample", {})
   244	    frida_cfg = cfg.get("lab_environment", {}).get("telemetry", {}).get("frida", {})
   245	
   246	    package_name = args.package or sample_cfg.get("package_name") or "com.example.malware"
   247	    port = args.port or frida_cfg.get("port", 38921)
   248	    duration = args.duration
   249	
   250	    script_paths = [
   251	        "instrumentation/agent/bypass_antidebug.js",
   252	        "instrumentation/agent/crypto_dumper.js",
   253	        "instrumentation/agent/socket_tracer.js"
   254	    ]
   255	
   256	    loader = FridaLoader(
   257	        package_name=package_name,
   258	        port=port,
   259	        log_path=args.output,
   260	        dry_run=args.dry_run
   261	    )
   262	
   263	    def sig_handler(sig, frame):
   264	        print("\n[!] Signal received. Shutting down instrumentation...")
   265	        loader.cleanup()
   266	        sys.exit(0)
   267	
   268	    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, sig_handler)
   269	    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, sig_handler)
   270	
   271	    loader.run(script_paths, duration=duration)
   272	
   273	
   274	if __name__ == "__main__":
   275	    main()
