FILE: ./task-step2.md
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1	# Phase 2 Plan & Task Breakdown: On-Device Stealth Cloaking (`task-step2.md`)
     2	
     3	## 1. Phase Objective & Operational Mandate
     4	The goal of **Phase 2 (On-Device Stealth Cloaking)** is to maintain active USB debugging (`adbd`) and host automation capabilities while making the Android OS report a completely stock, un-debugged production state to user-space applications.
     5	
     6	Modern Android infostealers actively query multiple system layers to detect dynamic instrumentation environments:
     7	1. **System Properties:** Inspecting `ro.debuggable`, `ro.secure`, `sys.usb.state`, `sys.usb.config`, and `init.svc.adbd`.
     8	2. **Java SettingsProvider:** Querying `Settings.Global.ADB_ENABLED` and `DEVELOPMENT_SETTINGS_ENABLED`.
     9	3. **Network Interfaces & Proxies:** Detecting `tun0`/`ppp0` VPN interfaces and `http.proxyHost` properties.
    10	4. **Local Network Ports:** Connecting to or scanning `/proc/net/tcp` for default Frida port `27042` (`:69A2`) or remote ADB port `5555`.
    11	
    12	If any of these indicators are exposed, the malware terminates execution or emits decoy traffic, blinding the analyst to true C2 infrastructure.
    13	
    14	---
    15	
    16	## 2. Tasks for Phase 2
    17	
    18	| Task ID | Component | Description | Deliverable |
    19	| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
    20	| **T2.1** | `device_cloaking/apply_stealth_props.sh` | Shell script for device root (`su -c`) using Magisk/KernelSU `resetprop` to mask properties in memory. Supports `apply`, `restore`, and `status`. | `device_cloaking/apply_stealth_props.sh` |
    21	| **T2.2** | `device_cloaking/check_lsposed_devmode.sh` | Diagnostic script auditing `system_server` Binder spoofing and LSPosed `HideDevMode` status. | `device_cloaking/check_lsposed_devmode.sh` |
    22	| **T2.3** | `device_cloaking/verify_stealth_state.py` | Host-side pre-flight audit asserting 5 cloaking layers before malware launch. Includes `--dry-run`, `--push-and-apply`, and `--restore`. | `device_cloaking/verify_stealth_state.py` |
    23	| **T2.4** | Framework Ledgers | Update `docs/steps.md`, `LOG.md`, and `FIXES.md` with engineering rationale and test results. | `docs/steps.md`, `LOG.md`, `FIXES.md` |
    24	
    25	---
    26	
    27	## 3. Technical Specifications per Task
    28	
    29	### Task 2.1: `device_cloaking/apply_stealth_props.sh`
    30	- **Execution Environment:** Android shell with root privileges (`su -c`).
    31	- **Binary Discovery:** Detects `resetprop` in standard PATH, `/data/adb/ksu/bin/resetprop` (KernelSU), `/data/adb/ap/bin/resetprop` (APatch), or `/system/bin/resetprop` (Magisk).
    32	- **Target Properties (`apply`):**
    33	  - `ro.debuggable 0`
    34	  - `ro.secure 1`
    35	  - `ro.build.type user`
    36	  - `ro.build.tags release-keys`
    37	  - `sys.usb.state mtp`
    38	  - `sys.usb.config mtp`
    39	  - `persist.sys.usb.config mtp`
    40	  - `init.svc.adbd stopped` (masks property while leaving daemon socket alive)
    41	- **Target Properties (`restore`):**
    42	  - `ro.debuggable 1`
    43	  - `sys.usb.state adb`
    44	  - `sys.usb.config adb`
    45	  - `init.svc.adbd running`
    46	- **Status Mode (`status`):** Audits current property values and reports PASS/FAIL status table.
    47	
    48	### Task 2.2: `device_cloaking/check_lsposed_devmode.sh`
    49	- **Objective:** Verify that `Settings.Global.getInt("adb_enabled")` and `"development_settings_enabled"` return `0` at the `system_server` IPC level without injecting in-process hooks into the target malware.
    50	- **Checks:**
    51	  1. Inspects `/data/adb/lspd` and `/data/adb/modules` for LSPosed / Zygisk framework status.
    52	  2. Queries `settings get global adb_enabled` and `settings get global development_settings_enabled`.
    53	  3. Verifies `content query --uri content://settings/global --where "name='adb_enabled'"` from unprivileged contexts.
    54	  4. Returns structured diagnostic output with clear remediation instructions if LSPosed `HideDevMode` is inactive.
    55	
    56	### Task 2.3: `device_cloaking/verify_stealth_state.py`
    57	- **Host Execution:** Python 3 standard library with optional PyYAML support.
    58	- **Pre-Flight Audit Layers:**
    59	  1. **Layer 1: System Properties:** `ro.debuggable == 0`, `ro.secure == 1`, `sys.usb.state == mtp`, `init.svc.adbd == stopped`.
    60	  2. **Layer 2: SettingsProvider:** `Settings.Global.adb_enabled in ('0', 'null', '')`.
    61	  3. **Layer 3: Network Hygiene:** Zero `tun`, `ppp`, or `p2p` interfaces; `http.proxyHost` empty.
    62	  4. **Layer 4: Port Exposure:** Port `27042` (`:69A2`) absent from `/proc/net/tcp` and `/proc/net/tcp6`.
    63	  5. **Layer 5: ADB Transport Resilience:** Confirms ADB responds despite masked properties.
    64	- **Automation CLI:**
    65	  - `--config <path>`: Path to `config/lab_config.yaml`.
    66	  - `--serial <id>`: Target ADB serial.
    67	  - `--dry-run`: Simulation mode for automated testing without physical hardware.
    68	  - `--push-and-apply`: Pushes `apply_stealth_props.sh` to device and executes it via `su -c` before auditing.
    69	  - `--restore`: Pushes and executes restore command on device.
    70	
    71	---
    72	
    73	## 4. Verification & Gate G1 Criteria
    74	- **Pass Condition:** `python3 device_cloaking/verify_stealth_state.py` exits with code `0`.
    75	- **Fail Condition:** Any property mismatch, detected VPN interface, active proxy, or exposed Frida port causes exit code `1` and prints exact violation.
    76	- **Teardown / Restore:** Verified that `--restore` returns device to developer state.

FILE: ./task-step3.md
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1	# Phase 3 Plan & Task Breakdown: Zero-Footprint Network & TLS Interception (`task-step3.md`)
     2	
     3	## 1. Phase Objective & Operational Mandate
     4	The goal of **Phase 3 (Gate G3)** is to intercept 100% of out-of-band network traffic (DNS lookups, raw TCP/UDP flows, and plaintext TLS sessions) without setting an Android system proxy (`http.proxyHost`), without installing untrusted user CA certificates, and without creating VPN (`tun0`) interfaces on the physical device.
     5	
     6	Modern infostealers evade network analysis by:
     7	1. **Detecting System Proxies:** Reading `http.proxyHost` or calling `ConnectivityManager.getDefaultProxy()`.
     8	2. **Checking for VPN Interception:** Scanning `NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces()` for `tun0` or `ppp0`.
     9	3. **Enforcing TLS Pinning:** Using OkHttp `CertificatePinner` or custom native trust stores, aborting handshakes if an untrusted proxy CA is encountered.
    10	
    11	### Stealth Countermeasure Hierarchy
    12	- **Network Layer:** Transparent external routing via a dedicated Linux Wi-Fi Access Point (`ap0`) with `iptables` NAT masquerade and raw `tcpdump` capture.
    13	- **Crypto Layer:** Linux kernel eBPF uprobes on Conscrypt's `libssl.so` using `eCapture` on `wlan0`. Extracts TLS master secrets (`sslkey.log`) and decrypted packet payloads (`tls_decrypted.pcapng`) directly from kernel space with zero memory modification inside the malware address space.
    14	
    15	---
    16	
    17	## 2. Tasks for Phase 3
    18	
    19	| Task ID | Component | Description | Deliverable |
    20	| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
    21	| **T3.1** | `network_capture/gateway_setup.sh` | Linux Wi-Fi AP (`ap0`) configuration, hostapd, dnsmasq, iptables NAT, and background tcpdump capture. Supports `start`, `stop`, `status`, and `--dry-run`. | `network_capture/gateway_setup.sh` |
    22	| **T3.2** | `network_capture/ecapture_runner.py` | Python host controller managing static ARM64 `ecapture` eBPF uprobes on `/apex/com.android.conscrypt/lib64/libssl.so`. Emits `sslkey.log` and `tls_decrypted.pcapng`. | `network_capture/ecapture_runner.py` |
    23	| **T3.3** | Graceful Teardown & Buffer Flushing | Implements signal handlers (SIGINT/SIGTERM), syncs device eBPF buffers, and pulls capture artifacts to the host workstation. | Integrated in `ecapture_runner.py` & `gateway_setup.sh` |
    24	| **T3.4** | `network_capture/sinkhole_inetsim.conf` | Isolated DNS and HTTP/HTTPS sinkhole configuration for safe C2 emulation in offline lab environments. | `network_capture/sinkhole_inetsim.conf` |
    25	| **T3.5** | Framework Ledgers | Update `docs/steps.md`, `LOG.md`, and `FIXES.md` with engineering rationale and verification results. | `docs/steps.md`, `LOG.md`, `FIXES.md` |
    26	
    27	---
    28	
    29	## 3. Technical Specifications
    30	
    31	### Task 3.1: `network_capture/gateway_setup.sh`
    32	- **Host Dependencies:** Linux host (or remote Linux AP gateway) with `hostapd`, `dnsmasq`, `iptables`, `tcpdump`.
    33	- **Interface Parameters:** AP interface `ap0` (IP: `192.168.44.1`), upstream interface `eth0`, subnet `192.168.44.0/24`.
    34	- **Capture File:** Outputs `raw_traffic.pcap` with full packet payloads (`-s 0`).
    35	- **Dry-run Mode:** `--dry-run` flag prints exact commands for macOS or non-Linux host testing.
    36	
    37	### Task 3.2: `network_capture/ecapture_runner.py`
    38	- **Device Requirements:** Android 12-14 (ARM64), Linux kernel 5.4+ with eBPF and uprobes enabled.
    39	- **Binary Target:** `/data/local/tmp/ecapture`.
    40	- **Target Library:** `/apex/com.android.conscrypt/lib64/libssl.so` (with fallback to 32-bit `/apex/com.android.conscrypt/lib/libssl.so`).
    41	- **CLI Commands:** `start`, `stop`, `status`, `pull`.
    42	- **Simulation:** Full `--dry-run` support for automated host testing.
    43	
    44	---
    45	
    46	## 4. Verification & Gate G3 Criteria
    47	- **Pass Condition:**
    48	  1. `gateway_setup.sh start` successfully initializes routing and begins `tcpdump` on `ap0`.
    49	  2. `ecapture_runner.py start` attaches eBPF uprobes to Conscrypt `libssl.so`.
    50	  3. `ecapture_runner.py stop --pull-dir <dir>` cleanly shuts down eCapture via SIGINT/SIGTERM, flushes buffers, and pulls `sslkey.log` and `tls_decrypted.pcapng`.

     1	# Phase 4 Plan & Task Breakdown: Dynamic Runtime & Pre-Network Crypto Instrumentation (`task-step4.md`)
     2	
     3	## 1. Phase Objective & Operational Mandate
     4	The goal of **Phase 4 (Gate G4)** is to intercept pre-network cryptographic operations (AES, DES, RC4, XOR) applied to stolen canary credentials before payloads enter the TLS tunnel, and trace direct low-level native socket connections that bypass standard Java HTTP APIs.
     5	
     6	Modern infostealers frequently encrypt stolen mock data in memory using symmetric ciphers prior to transmission, or communicate directly via native C2 sockets (`connect()`, `send()`). If analysts only capture encrypted packets or TLS streams, the adversary's encryption key and inner protocol format remain unknown.
     7	
     8	### In-Process Hooking Guardrails
     9	- **Minimal Footprint:** In-process hooks are surgical and restricted to essential primitives (`Cipher.init()`, `Cipher.doFinal()`, `connect()`, `send()`). Monolithic framework hooking is forbidden to avoid timing delays and anti-hooking heuristics.
    10	- **Port Masking:** Frida server executes on non-standard port `38921` (masking default `27042`) under a renamed process (`hl_daemon`).
    11	- **Telemetry Streaming:** All captured buffers, secret keys, IVs, and socket connections are emitted asynchronously via Frida `send()` and streamed to `instrumentation_events.jsonl` on the host.
    12	
    13	---
    14	
    15	## 2. Tasks for Phase 4
    16	
    17	| Task ID | Component | Description | Deliverable |
    18	| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
    19	| **T4.1** | `instrumentation/agent/bypass_antidebug.js` | In-process fallback hooks for `Settings.Global`, `android.os.Debug`, native `libc.so` `__system_property_get`, and `/proc/self/status` `TracerPid`. | `instrumentation/agent/bypass_antidebug.js` |
    20	| **T4.2** | `instrumentation/agent/crypto_dumper.js` | Intercepts `javax.crypto.Cipher` init and doFinal. Dumps algorithm, key bytes, IV bytes, input plaintext, and output ciphertext as hex. | `instrumentation/agent/crypto_dumper.js` |
    21	| **T4.3** | `instrumentation/agent/socket_tracer.js` | Hooks `InetAddress.getByName()`, `java.net.URL`, and native `libc.so` `connect()` / `send()` / `sendto()` for IPv4 and IPv6 direct C2 beacons. | `instrumentation/agent/socket_tracer.js` |
    22	| **T4.4** | `instrumentation/frida_loader.py` | Python host loader spawning target APK over custom port `38921`, loading consolidated agent scripts, and writing JSON telemetry to disk. Includes `--dry-run`. | `instrumentation/frida_loader.py` |
    23	| **T4.5** | Framework Ledgers | Update `docs/steps.md`, `LOG.md`, and `FIXES.md` with engineering rationale and verification results. | `docs/steps.md`, `LOG.md`, `FIXES.md` |
    24	
    25	---
    26	
    27	## 3. Technical Specifications
    28	
    29	### Task 4.1: `instrumentation/agent/bypass_antidebug.js`
    30	- **Java Layer:** Overloads `Settings.Global.getInt` for `adb_enabled` and `development_settings_enabled` returning `0`.
    31	- **Debug APIs:** Overrides `android.os.Debug.isDebuggerConnected()` and `waitingForDebugger()` returning `false`.
    32	- **Native Layer:** Intercepts `__system_property_get` in `libc.so` for `ro.debuggable` (writes `"0"`) and `sys.usb.state` (writes `"mtp"`).
    33	- **Procfs Cloaking:** Intercepts `openat` / `read` calls targeting `/proc/self/status` to mask `TracerPid` as `0`.
    34	
    35	### Task 4.2: `instrumentation/agent/crypto_dumper.js`
    36	- **Target APIs:** `javax.crypto.Cipher.init(int opmode, Key key, ...)` and `javax.crypto.Cipher.doFinal(byte[] input)`.
    37	- **Telemetry Payload:**
    38	  - `event`: `"CRYPTO_INIT"` and `"CRYPTO_DOFINAL"`.
    39	  - `algorithm`: e.g., `"AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding"`.
    40	  - `opmode`: `"ENCRYPT_MODE"` (1) vs `"DECRYPT_MODE"` (2).
    41	  - `key_hex`: Extracted secret key bytes.
    42	  - `iv_hex`: Extracted initialization vector.
    43	  - `input_hex` & `output_hex`: Complete pre-encryption and post-encryption byte arrays.
    44	
    45	### Task 4.3: `instrumentation/agent/socket_tracer.js`
    46	- **Native Target:** `libc.so` exports `connect`, `send`, `sendto`.
    47	- **Java Target:** `java.net.InetAddress.getByName`, `java.net.URL.<init>`.
    48	- **Telemetry Payload:**
    49	  - `event`: `"SOCKET_CONNECT"`, `"SOCKET_SEND"`, `"DNS_LOOKUP"`.
    50	  - Destination IP, port, URL string, protocol family (`AF_INET`, `AF_INET6`).
    51	
    52	### Task 4.4: `instrumentation/frida_loader.py`
    53	- **Host Execution:** Python 3 standard library + optional `frida` package.
    54	- **Port Management:** Connects to `127.0.0.1:38921` via `frida.get_device_manager().add_remote_device(...)`.
    55	- **Simulation Mode:** `--dry-run` simulates spawn, agent compilation, and generates sample `instrumentation_events.jsonl` without requiring an active device.
    56	
    57	---
    58	
    59	## 4. Verification & Gate G4 Criteria
    60	- **Pass Condition:**
    61	  1. `frida_loader.py` cleanly connects on custom port `38921` and spawns target package.
    62	  2. Injected agents emit structured JSON events to `instrumentation_events.jsonl`.
    63	  3. Pre-encryption canary strings are successfully captured in `CRYPTO_DOFINAL` input buffers.

FILE: ./task-step5.md

     1	# Phase 5 Plan & Task Breakdown: Traffic Decryption, Canary Correlation & IOC Reporting (`task-step5.md`)
     2	
     3	## 1. Phase Objective & Operational Mandate
     4	The goal of **Phase 5 (Gate G5)** is to automatically correlate what the malware stole in memory with the exact remote server it contacted.
     5	
     6	We bridge two independent observation channels:
     7	1. **Memory Channel (Frida `instrumentation_events.jsonl`):** Pre-encryption buffers, secret keys, IVs, and plaintext tokens harvested from `Cipher.doFinal()`.
     8	2. **Network Channel (`raw_traffic.pcap` + `sslkey.log` via eCapture):** Decrypted TLS streams and raw TCP packets captured out-of-band on the gateway.
     9	
    10	By running a multi-encoding pattern search (raw, base64, hex, URL-encoded) against both channels using `config/mock_canaries.json`, the sandbox provides indisputable evidence of data theft, identifies exfiltration channels (Telegram, HTTP POST, direct socket), and generates standardized STIX 2.1 threat intelligence bundles.
    11	
    12	---
    13	
    14	## 2. Tasks for Phase 5
    15	
    16	| Task ID | Component | Description | Deliverable |
    17	| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
    18	| **T5.1** | `parser_correlator/pcap_decryptor.py` | Combines raw gateway PCAP and eCapture `sslkey.log` using `tshark` / `pyshark` to extract plaintext HTTP/1.1 and HTTP/2 flows. | `parser_correlator/pcap_decryptor.py` |
    19	| **T5.2** | `parser_correlator/canary_hunter.py` | Multi-encoding canary correlation engine scanning crypto dumps and decrypted traffic for mock data leakage. | `parser_correlator/canary_hunter.py` |
    20	| **T5.3** | `parser_correlator/ioc_extractor.py` | Normalizes C2 endpoints, domains, and tokens into structured JSON, STIX 2.1 bundles, and executive Markdown reports. | `parser_correlator/ioc_extractor.py` |
    21	| **T5.4** | `orchestrator.py` | Master CLI tying all gates (G0-G5) into an automated end-to-end analysis workflow (`python3 orchestrator.py --apk sample.apk --duration 120`). | `orchestrator.py` |
    22	| **T5.5** | Framework Ledgers & Summaries | Create `completed.md`, `problem_solution.md`, and update `docs/steps.md`, `LOG.md`, `FIXES.md`. | Ledgers |
    23	
    24	---
    25	
    26	## 3. Technical Specifications
    27	
    28	### Task 5.1: `parser_correlator/pcap_decryptor.py`
    29	- **Decryption Engine:** Executes `tshark` with `-o tls.keylog_file:<sslkey.log>` to decrypt TLS records.
    30	- **Extracted Fields:** Frame timestamp, source IP/port, destination IP/port, HTTP method, URI, request headers, POST body data (`http.file_data`).
    31	- **Resilience:** If `tshark` is not installed or the PCAP is empty, provides a graceful fallback parser so the pipeline continues processing without unhandled exceptions.
    32	
    33	### Task 5.2: `parser_correlator/canary_hunter.py`
    34	- **Target Ingestion:** Reads all synthetic credentials from `config/mock_canaries.json`.
    35	- **Encoding Permutations:** Expands each canary into raw UTF-8, Base64, Hexadecimal, and URL-encoded variants.
    36	- **Search Scope:**
    37	  1. Frida crypto events: `input_utf8`, `input_hex`, `output_utf8`.
    38	  2. Decrypted network requests: POST bodies, query strings, headers.
    39	- **Hit Record:** Timestamp, canary category, token name, encoding matched, channel (Memory vs Network), destination IP/URL.
    40	
    41	### Task 5.3: `parser_correlator/ioc_extractor.py`
    42	- **Observables:** C2 IPv4 addresses, domains, ports, URLs, Telegram bot tokens, cryptocurrency addresses.
    43	- **Outputs:**
    44	  1. `iocs.json`: Machine-readable summary of all indicators.
    45	  2. `threat_report_stix21.json`: STIX 2.1 bundle with `malware`, `infrastructure`, `ipv4-addr`, `domain-name`, and `relationship` objects.
    46	  3. `analysis_report.md`: Executive threat report with evidence tags (`[C]`, `[D]`, `[M]`, `[N]`, `[E]`).
    47	
    48	### Task 5.4: `orchestrator.py`
    49	- **Workflow Pipeline:**
    50	  1. Load config & sample metadata (`lab_config.yaml`).
    51	  2. Run pre-flight stealth audit (`verify_stealth_state.py`).
    52	  3. Seed mock canaries (`seed_canaries.py`).
    53	  4. Start gateway capture (`gateway_setup.sh start`) and eBPF (`ecapture_runner.py start`).
    54	  5. Spawn malware with Frida instrumentation (`frida_loader.py`).
    55	  6. Countdown observation window.
    56	  7. Teardown: terminate malware, flush and pull PCAPs/keylogs, clean canaries (`seed_canaries.py --clean`).
    57	  8. Correlate: run `pcap_decryptor.py`, `canary_hunter.py`, `ioc_extractor.py`.
    58	  9. Print executive findings summary to console.
    59	- **Offline Simulation:** `--dry-run` simulates the entire analysis lifecycle from start to finish.
    60	
    61	---
    62	
    63	## 4. Verification & Gate G5 Criteria
    64	- **Pass Condition:**
    65	  1. `pcap_decryptor.py` outputs decrypted HTTP flow records.
    66	  2. `canary_hunter.py` identifies canary tokens in memory dumps and network streams.
    67	  3. `ioc_extractor.py` outputs valid STIX 2.1 JSON and Markdown report.
    68	  4. `orchestrator.py --dry-run` completes all gates G1 through G5 end-to-end with exit code 0.

     1	# Phase 6 Plan & Task Breakdown: First-Class Commercial AppSec & Privacy Auditing (`task-step6.md`)
     2	
     3	## 1. Phase Objective & Architectural Mandate
     4	
     5	The objective of **Phase 6** is to elevate `dbug` from a malware-only dynamic analysis sandbox into a **universal stealth mobile security toolkit** supporting three first-class operational profiles:
     6	1. **`malware` (Threat Intelligence & Defense):** C2 extraction, bot token harvesting, STIX 2.1 threat bundle, YARA generation, malware family attribution.
     7	2. **`privacy` (Data Leakage & Regulatory Compliance):** Automated auditing of PII, contacts, SMS, and clipboard leakage to third-party ad/tracking SDKs (Meta, Google, AppsFlyer, Adjust, ByteDance) for GDPR, CCPA, and App Store review compliance.
     8	3. **`appsec` (Mobile Application Penetration Testing & API Audit):** Zero-trace API endpoint discovery, OpenAPI 3.0 export, OWASP MASVS cryptographic audit (ECB modes, static IVs, weak ciphers), and interactive login testing against heavily protected commercial apps (fintech, banking, e-commerce) without tripping commercial RASP / anti-tamper protections.
     9	
    10	---
    11	
    12	## 2. Tasks for Phase 6
    13	
    14	| Task ID | Component | Description | Deliverables |
    15	| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
    16	| **T6.1** | `config/tracker_catalog.json` | Knowledge base of third-party mobile advertising, analytics, telemetry, and tracking SDK domains. | `config/tracker_catalog.json` |
    17	| **T6.2** | `parser_correlator/privacy_auditor.py` | Privacy auditor correlating canary leakage and decrypted network flows against third-party tracker domains. | `parser_correlator/privacy_auditor.py`<br>`PRIVACY_AUDIT.md`<br>`privacy_findings.json` |
    18	| **T6.3** | `parser_correlator/appsec_auditor.py` | AppSec auditor analyzing cryptographic hygiene (OWASP MASVS) and generating OpenAPI 3.0 API specifications. | `parser_correlator/appsec_auditor.py`<br>`API_SECURITY_REPORT.md`<br>`openapi_spec.json` |
    19	| **T6.4** | `orchestrator.py` Profile & Interactive Engine | Introduces `--profile {malware,privacy,appsec,all}` and `--interactive` mode for manual tester interaction. | `orchestrator.py` |
    20	| **T6.5** | Automated Test Suite Extension | Unit & integration tests in `tests/test_framework_e2e.py` verifying privacy, appsec, and multi-profile workflows. | `tests/test_framework_e2e.py` |
    21	| **T6.6** | Framework Ledgers & Documentation | Update `docs/steps.md`, `completed.md`, `problem_solution.md`, `FIXES.md`, and `LOG.md`. | Ledgers |
    22	
    23	---
    24	
    25	## 3. Technical Specifications
    26	
    27	### Task 6.1: `config/tracker_catalog.json`
    28	- **Scope:** Curated taxonomy of top mobile tracking, advertising, and analytics networks:
    29	  - **Advertising & Attribution:** AppsFlyer, Adjust, Branch.io, Kochava, Meta/Facebook Graph, Google Ads/AdMob, Unity Ads, ByteDance/Pangle.
    30	  - **Analytics & Telemetry:** Google Firebase/App-Measurement, Mixpanel, Amplitude, Flurry, Segment, CleverTap.
    31	  - **Crash & Performance:** Sentry, Firebase Crashlytics, Bugsnag, New Relic.
    32	- **Fields per entry:** Domain suffixes, company/network name, tracker category, privacy sensitivity score.
    33	
    34	### Task 6.2: `parser_correlator/privacy_auditor.py`
    35	- **Correlation Logic:**
    36	  1. Inspects all decrypted HTTP/TLS requests from `decrypted_http_requests.jsonl`.
    37	  2. Resolves destination domains against `config/tracker_catalog.json` to classify destinations into First-Party API vs Third-Party Tracker vs Crash Reporter.
    38	  3. Correlates `CANARY_EXFIL_HITS.json` to determine what sensitive data (contacts, SMS OTPs, clipboard, wallet keys) was transmitted to third-party tracking entities.
    39	- **Outputs:**
    40	  - `privacy_findings.json`: Machine-readable audit detailing tracked SDKs, transmitted data types, and risk levels.
    41	  - `PRIVACY_AUDIT.md`: Executive compliance report citing GDPR Article 5/6 principles, California CCPA data disclosure violations, and App Store privacy manifest discrepancies.
    42	
    43	### Task 6.3: `parser_correlator/appsec_auditor.py`
    44	- **OWASP MASVS Cryptographic Audit (MASVS-CRYPTO):**
    45	  - Analyzes `instrumentation_events.jsonl` from `crypto_dumper.js`.
    46	  - Flags insecure cipher modes: AES/ECB (no IV), static hardcoded IVs (e.g. all zeros or sequential bytes), DES/3DES, MD5/SHA1 hashing.
    47	  - Reports secret key reuse and plain HTTP fallbacks.
    48	- **API Inventory & OpenAPI 3.0 Export (MASVS-NETWORK):**
    49	  - Aggregates unique HTTP endpoints, methods (`GET`, `POST`, `PUT`, `DELETE`), headers, query parameters, and request body JSON schemas.
    50	  - Emits a valid OpenAPI 3.0 JSON specification (`openapi_spec.json`) suitable for import into Postman, Burp Suite, or OWASP ZAP.
    51	  - Emits `API_SECURITY_REPORT.md`.
    52	
    53	### Task 6.4: `orchestrator.py` Profile & Interactive Engine
    54	- **CLI Options:**
    55	  - `--profile {malware,privacy,appsec,all}`: Defaults to `malware`.
    56	  - `--interactive`: Instead of terminating after a fixed `--duration`, maintains zero-trace capture alive indefinitely while presenting interactive instructions to the analyst. Stops cleanly on `Ctrl+C` or Enter key and runs post-processing.
    57	- **Target Reconciliation:** Synchronizes profile deliverables to `targets/<target_id>/`:
    58	  - If `--profile privacy` or `all`: generates `PRIVACY_AUDIT.md` and `privacy_findings.json`.
    59	  - If `--profile appsec` or `all`: generates `API_SECURITY_REPORT.md` and `openapi_spec.json`.
    60	  - If `--profile malware` or `all`: generates `C2_PROTOCOL.md`, `threat_report_stix21.json`, YARA rule, and `FAMILY_ATTRIBUTION.json`.
    61	
    62	---
    63	
    64	## 4. Verification & Validation Criteria
    65	1. `config/tracker_catalog.json` validates against JSON schema.
    66	2. `privacy_auditor.py` correctly classifies first-party vs third-party endpoints and flags PII leakage.
    67	3. `appsec_auditor.py` detects weak crypto algorithms and outputs valid OpenAPI 3.0 JSON.
    68	4. `orchestrator.py --profile all --dry-run` executes all stages without failure.
    69	5. All 14+ automated tests in `tests/test_framework_e2e.py` pass with exit code 0.

FILE: ./tests/test_framework_e2e.py

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""test_framework_e2e.py: Comprehensive Automated End-to-End Test Suite for dbug.
     3	
     4	Verifies all pipeline stages, tool interfaces, defensive telemetry generators,
     5	parsers, and threat intelligence exporters across Gates G0 through G5.
     6	Designed to run in offline/CI environments with 100% test reproducibility.
     7	"""
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import os
    11	import re
    12	import shutil
    13	import subprocess
    14	import sys
    15	import tempfile
    16	import unittest
    17	
    18	
    19	PYTHON_BIN = sys.executable
    20	WORKSPACE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    21	
    22	
    23	class TestDbugFramework(unittest.TestCase):
    24	
    25	    def run_cmd(self, cmd_args, check=True):
    26	        """Helper to run a shell command inside the dbug workspace."""
    27	        res = subprocess.run(
    28	            cmd_args,
    29	            cwd=WORKSPACE_DIR,
    30	            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
    31	            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
    32	            text=True
    33	        )
    34	        if check and res.returncode != 0:
    35	            print(f"FAILED: {' '.join(cmd_args)}\nSTDOUT:\n{res.stdout}\nSTDERR:\n{res.stderr}", file=sys.stderr)
    36	        return res
    37	
    38	    def test_01_canary_seeding_dry_run(self):
    39	        """Gate G2: Test canary data injection, verification, and cleaning simulation."""
    40	        # Test Seeding
    41	        res = self.run_cmd([PYTHON_BIN, "config/seed_canaries.py", "--dry-run"])
    42	        self.assertEqual(res.returncode, 0)
    43	        self.assertIn("Total canary contacts seeded", res.stdout)
    44	        self.assertIn("Canary seeding sequence finished successfully", res.stdout)
    45	
    46	        # Test Verification
    47	        res_v = self.run_cmd([PYTHON_BIN, "config/seed_canaries.py", "--verify", "--dry-run"])
    48	        self.assertEqual(res_v.returncode, 0)
    49	        self.assertIn("[DRY-RUN] Verification simulated SUCCESS", res_v.stdout)
    50	
    51	        # Test Cleaning
    52	        res_c = self.run_cmd([PYTHON_BIN, "config/seed_canaries.py", "--clean", "--dry-run"])
    53	        self.assertEqual(res_c.returncode, 0)
    54	        self.assertIn("Cleanup complete: Synthetic contacts, SMS, and clipboard cleared", res_c.stdout)
    55	
    56	    def test_02_device_cloaking_preflight(self):
    57	        """Gate G1: Test 5-layer stealth pre-flight audit in dry-run mode."""
    58	        res = self.run_cmd([PYTHON_BIN, "device_cloaking/verify_stealth_state.py", "--dry-run"])
    59	        self.assertEqual(res.returncode, 0)
    60	        self.assertIn("ALL PRE-FLIGHT STEALTH CHECKS PASSED", res.stdout)
    61	        self.assertIn("Layer 1: Auditing System Properties", res.stdout)
    62	        self.assertIn("Layer 2: Auditing Settings.Global Developer Flags", res.stdout)
    63	        self.assertIn("Layer 3: Auditing Network Hygiene", res.stdout)
    64	        self.assertIn("Layer 4: Auditing Port Exposure", res.stdout)
    65	        self.assertIn("Layer 5: Verifying ADB Transport Viability", res.stdout)
    66	
    67	    def test_03_static_triage_apk(self):
    68	        """Gate G0: Test static APK/DEX unpacking and anti-analysis auditor."""
    69	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_dir:
    70	            res = self.run_cmd([
    71	                PYTHON_BIN, "sample_triage/triage_apk.py",
    72	                "--apk", "samples/sample.apk",
    73	                "--package", "com.example.malware",

   135	    def test_09_canary_hunter_multi_encoding(self):
   136	        """Gate G5: Test multi-encoding canary correlation against mock data."""
   137	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_dir:
   138	            hits_file = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "CANARY_EXFIL_HITS.json")
   139	            events_file = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "events.jsonl")
   140	            traffic_file = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "traffic.jsonl")
   141	
   142	            # Write test synthetic event with base64 encoded canary
   143	            with open(events_file, "w") as f:
   144	                f.write(json.dumps({
   145	                    "timestamp": 1729080000.0,
   146	                    "event": "CIPHER_DOFINAL",
   147	                    "algorithm": "AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding",
   148	                    "plaintext_hex": "43414e4152595f534d535f4f54505f39393231" # CANARY_SMS_OTP_9921
   149	                }) + "\n")
   150	
   151	            with open(traffic_file, "w") as f:
   152	                f.write(json.dumps({
   153	                    "timestamp": 1729080001.0,
   154	                    "src_ip": "10.42.0.50",
   155	                    "dst_ip": "198.51.100.24",
   156	                    "payload": "user_otp=Q0FOQVJZX1NNU19PVFBfOTkyMQ==" # base64 CANARY_SMS_OTP_9921
   157	                }) + "\n")
   158	
   159	            res = self.run_cmd([
   160	                PYTHON_BIN, "parser_correlator/canary_hunter.py",
   161	                "--canaries", "config/mock_canaries.json",
   162	                "--events", events_file,
   163	                "--traffic", traffic_file,
   164	                "--output", hits_file
   165	            ])
   166	            self.assertEqual(res.returncode, 0)
   167	            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(hits_file))
   168	            with open(hits_file, "r") as f:
   169	                data = json.load(f)
   170	                self.assertGreater(data.get("total_exfiltration_hits", 0), 0)
   171	                self.assertGreater(len(data.get("hits", [])), 0)
   172	
   173	    def test_10_c2_protocol_decoder(self):
   174	        """Gate G5: Test C2 framing reconstruction and specification generation."""
   175	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_dir:
   176	            traffic_file = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "decrypted_http_requests.jsonl")
   177	            with open(traffic_file, "w") as f:
   178	                f.write(json.dumps({
   179	                    "uri": "https://api.telegram.org/bot6829104812:AAH9fCANARYTokenMockSecretKey/sendMessage",
   180	                    "method": "POST",
   181	                    "payload": '{"cmd":"bot_register","device":"pixel7"}'
   182	                }) + "\n")
   183	
   184	            proto_doc = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "C2_PROTOCOL.md")
   185	            res = self.run_cmd([
   186	                PYTHON_BIN, "parser_correlator/c2_protocol_decoder.py",
   187	                "--session-dir", tmp_dir,
   188	                "--package", "com.example.malware",
   189	                "--output", proto_doc
   190	            ])
   191	            self.assertEqual(res.returncode, 0)
   192	            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(proto_doc))
   193	            with open(proto_doc, "r") as f:

   195	                self.assertIn("Command and Control (C2) Protocol Specification", content)
   196	                self.assertIn("Telegram Bot API", content)
